    76	const REC = (sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body) => [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body].join('\x00');
    77	const logStdout = (...recs) => recs.map((r) => r + '\x1e').join('');
    78	const CLEAN_LOG = logStdout(REC('c1', NAME, EMAIL, NAME, EMAIL, 'subtask work'));

    80	/**
    81	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
    82	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
    83	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
    84	 */
    85	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG } = {}) {
    86	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
    87	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
    88	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
    89	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
    90	
    91	  const list = () => {
    92	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
    93	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
    94	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
    95	  };
    96	
    97	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
    98	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
    99	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   100	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   101	
   102	    // Explicit overrides win.
   103	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   104	    if (okey) {
   105	      const r = over[okey];
   106	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   107	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   111	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   112	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   113	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   114	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   115	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); }
   116	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   117	    }
   118	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune') || argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   119	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) { if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1]); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   120	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   121	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   122	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${MAIN_SHA}\n`, stderr: '' });
   123	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   124	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   125	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   126	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   127	  };
   128	  fn.calls = calls;
   129	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   130	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   131	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   132	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
   133	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
   134	  return fn;
   135	}

   137	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   138	  const queue = [...script];
   139	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   140	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   141	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   142	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   143	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   144	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   145	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   146	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   147	    }
   148	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   149	  };
   150	  fn.calls = calls;
   151	  return fn;
   152	}

   154	function makePipeRepo({ spec = pipelineSpec(), config = PIPELINE_CONFIG, files = {}, git, runner, env = {} } = {}) {
   155	  const io = makeIo({
   156	    now: T0,
   157	    host: 'pipe-host',
   158	    pid: 4242,
   159	    startTime: 111000,
   160	    env,
   161	    files: {
   162	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   163	      '/repo/.git/HEAD': 'ref: refs/heads/main\n',
   164	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(config, null, 2),
   165	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   166	      ...files,
   167	    },
   168	  });
   169	  const g = git ?? pipelineGit();
   170	  g.setLockProbe(() => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK));
   171	  io.execFile = g;
   172	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   173	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   174	  io.__git = g;
   175	  io.__runner = fake;
   176	  return io;
   177	}

   181	const argsOf = (call) => (call?.args ?? []).map(String);
   182	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   183	const childWithModel = (runner, model) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).includes(model));

     1	/**
     2	 * Child supervision — the loop's reliability surface. Owns the FULL child
     3	 * lifecycle: argv assembly (per-role permissions), detached process-group
     4	 * spawn with closed stdin, timeout SIGTERM→grace→SIGKILL of the whole group,

    15	// Every review seat is read-only — writers are the implementer-side roles only.

    26	/**
    27	 * Assemble the spawn contract for a role child. Reviewers are read-only on
    28	 * every platform; every child is marked supervised (its own baton hooks
    29	 * no-op), carries the sole-author git identity, and has stdin CLOSED at
    30	 * spawn (the codex-exec stdin-hang field failure).
    31	 * @param {{platform: string, role: string, model: string, effort?: string | null, mode?: string}} assignment
    32	 * @param {string} prompt
    33	 * @param {{root: string}} opts
    34	 * @returns {{command: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>, stdio: any[]}}
    35	 */
    36	export function buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, opts) {
    37	  const readOnly = REVIEWER_ROLES.has(assignment.role);
    38	  const env = { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1', ...GIT_IDENTITY };
    39	  const stdio = ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'];
    40	
    41	  if (assignment.platform === 'claude-code') {
    42	    const args = ['-p', prompt];
    43	    if (readOnly) {
    44	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'default', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob');
    45	    } else {
    46	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'acceptEdits', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob,Write,Edit,Bash');
    47	    }
    48	    return { command: 'claude', args, env, stdio };
    49	  }
    50	
    51	  // codex (and the default shape for exec-style CLIs).
    52	  const args = ['exec', '-C', opts.root, '-s', readOnly ? 'read-only' : 'workspace-write', '--model', assignment.model];
    53	  if (assignment.effort) args.push('-c', `model_reasoning_effort=${assignment.effort}`);
    54	  args.push(prompt);
    55	  return { command: 'codex', args, env, stdio };
    56	}
    57	
    58	const VERDICT_RE = /VERDICT:\s*(APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|APPROVED|BLOCKED)\b/g;
    59	
    60	/** @param {string} region @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string} | null} */
    61	function lastVerdictIn(region) {
    62	  let last = null;
    63	  for (const m of region.matchAll(VERDICT_RE)) last = m;
    64	  if (!last) return null;
    65	  const after = region.slice((last.index ?? 0) + last[0].length);
    66	  return { verdict: last[1], findings: after.trim() };
    67	}
    68	
    69	/**
    70	 * Region-bounded verdict parsing — the prompt region (which quotes the
    71	 * verdict format in its instructions) is NEVER scanned, and there is no
    72	 * whole-transcript fallback. Unparseable is BLOCKED, never APPROVED.
    73	 * - codex: only the text after the LAST line starting with 'tokens used'.
    74	 * - claude-code: only the `result` field of the structured JSON output.
    75	 * Multiple tails inside the trusted region → the LAST one wins.
    76	 * @param {string} transcript @param {{platform: string}} opts
    77	 * @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string, reason?: string}}
    78	 */
    79	export function parseVerdict(transcript, { platform }) {
    80	  const unparseable = { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', reason: 'unparseable' };
    81	  const text = String(transcript ?? '');
    82	
    83	  if (platform === 'claude-code') {
    84	    let obj = null;
    85	    try {
    86	      obj = JSON.parse(text.trim());
    87	    } catch {
    88	      return unparseable;
    89	    }
    90	    if (typeof obj?.result !== 'string') return unparseable;
    91	    const hit = lastVerdictIn(obj.result);
    92	    return hit ?? unparseable;
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  // codex: locate the LAST 'tokens used' marker line; the trusted region is
    96	  // strictly after it.
    97	  const lines = text.split('\n');
    98	  let markerLine = -1;
    99	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
   100	    if (/^tokens used\b/i.test(lines[i])) markerLine = i;
   101	  }
   102	  if (markerLine === -1) return unparseable;
   103	  const region = lines.slice(markerLine + 1).join('\n');
   104	  const hit = lastVerdictIn(region);
   105	  return hit ?? unparseable;
   106	}
   107	
   108	const TRUNCATION_MARKER = '\n[... truncated by baton log cap ...]\n';
   109	
   110	/**
   111	 * Cap a transcript: head preserved, middle replaced with a marker, TAIL
   112	 * preserved verbatim (the verdict lives at the end and must never be cut).
   113	 * @param {string} text @param {number} maxBytes
   114	 * @returns {string}
   115	 */
   116	export function capLog(text, maxBytes) {
   117	  const s = String(text ?? '');
   118	  if (Buffer.byteLength(s) <= maxBytes) return s;
   119	  const headBytes = Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxBytes * 0.4));
   120	  const tailBytes = Math.max(1, maxBytes - headBytes);
   121	  const buf = Buffer.from(s);
   122	  const head = buf.subarray(0, headBytes).toString();
   123	  const tail = buf.subarray(buf.length - tailBytes).toString();
   124	  return head + TRUNCATION_MARKER + tail;
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * Exit classification delegates to core detect — one classifier, one
   129	 * signature table, no drift. Returns exactly classify(...).class.
   130	 * @param {string} transcript @param {number} exitCode @param {string} platform @param {any} table
   131	 * @returns {string}
   132	 */
   133	export function classifyChildExit(transcript, exitCode, platform, table) {
   134	  return classify({ text: transcript, exitCode, platform, table }).class;
   135	}
   136	
   137	/** @param {number} pid @param {NodeJS.Signals} sig */
   138	function killGroup(pid, sig) {
   139	  try {
   140	    process.kill(-pid, sig);
   141	  } catch {
   142	    // ESRCH — already gone.
   143	  }
   144	}
   145	
   146	/**
   147	 * Spawn and supervise one child: detached in its OWN process group, stdin
   148	 * closed; on timeout SIGTERM the group, wait graceMs, SIGKILL the group (no
   149	 * zombie grandchildren). Output streams to a capped log at opts.logPath; the
   150	 * verdict is parsed from the full transcript, region-bounded.
   151	 * @param {{command: string, args: string[], env?: Record<string, string>, cwd?: string}} spec
   152	 * @param {{timeoutMs: number, graceMs: number, logPath: string, maxLogBytes?: number, platform?: string}} opts
   153	 * @returns {Promise<{timedOut: boolean, exitCode: number | null, verdict?: string, findings?: string, reason?: string, logPath: string}>}
   154	 */
   155	export function superviseChild(spec, opts) {
   156	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
   157	    const maxLogBytes = opts.maxLogBytes ?? 1_000_000;
   158	    const child = spawn(spec.command, spec.args, {
   159	      cwd: spec.cwd,
   160	      env: { ...process.env, ...(spec.env ?? {}) },
   161	      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
   162	      detached: true, // its own process group — the kill unit
   163	    });
   164	
   165	    let out = '';
   166	    let timedOut = false;
   167	    /** @type {NodeJS.Timeout | null} */
   168	    let killTimer = null;
   169	
   170	    const onChunk = (/** @type {Buffer} */ c) => {
   171	      out += c.toString();
   172	    };
   173	    child.stdout?.on('data', onChunk);
   174	    child.stderr?.on('data', onChunk);
   175	
   176	    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
   177	      timedOut = true;
   178	      if (typeof child.pid === 'number') {
   179	        killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGTERM');
   180	        killTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   181	          if (typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
   182	        }, opts.graceMs);
   183	      }
   184	    }, opts.timeoutMs);
   185	
   186	    const finish = (/** @type {number | null} */ exitCode) => {
   187	      clearTimeout(timeout);
   188	      if (killTimer) clearTimeout(killTimer);
   189	      // Belt-and-braces: reap any group survivors even on a clean exit path.
   190	      if (timedOut && typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
   191	      try {
   192	        mkdirSync(dirname(opts.logPath), { recursive: true });
   193	        writeFileSync(opts.logPath, capLog(out, maxLogBytes));
   194	      } catch {
   195	        // The log must never mask the result.
   196	      }
   197	      const parsed = parseVerdict(out, { platform: opts.platform ?? 'codex' });
   198	      resolve({
   199	        timedOut,
   200	        exitCode: timedOut ? null : exitCode,
   201	        verdict: parsed.verdict,
   202	        findings: parsed.findings,
   203	        ...(parsed.reason ? { reason: parsed.reason } : {}),
   204	        logPath: opts.logPath,
   205	      });
   206	    };
   207	
   208	    child.on('error', () => finish(null));
   209	    child.on('close', (code) => finish(code));
   210	  });
   211	}

     1	import { jailRelPath } from '../util/jail.mjs';

     3	const RING_CAP = 500;

     5	/** Audit note for a refused payload — visible in the decision log, never a throw. */
     6	const refusalNote = (/** @type {string} */ type, /** @type {any} */ detail) =>
     7	  `[${type} with invalid payload refused: ${JSON.stringify(detail).slice(0, 120)}]`;
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Pure event reducer. Returns a new bundle; never mutates the input.
    11	 * Idempotent: an event whose dedupeKey is still in the bundle's ring is a no-op.
    12	 * Unknown event types degrade to a decision-log note — a checkpoint must never
    13	 * fail because a newer adapter emitted something this core doesn't know — and
    14	 * journal payloads are untrusted (gate-2 fix 6): malformed shapes degrade to a
    15	 * refusal note and repository paths are jailed before storage.
    16	 * @param {any} bundle
    17	 * @param {{seq: number, ts: string, type: string, dedupeKey: string, writerId?: string, source?: string, payload: any}} event
    18	 */
    19	export function applyEvent(bundle, event) {
    20	  // Journal lines are untrusted (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable null/number/
    21	  // string is a valid JSON line but not an event envelope — degrade to a
    22	  // no-op rather than crash replay. The store's load loop also pre-filters and
    23	  // warns; this is the last-line guard for any direct caller.
    24	  if (event === null || typeof event !== 'object' || Array.isArray(event)) return bundle;
    25	  if (bundle.dedupeRing.includes(event.dedupeKey)) return bundle;
    26	
    27	  const out = structuredClone(bundle);
    28	  const { seq, ts, type, payload = {} } = structuredClone(event);
    29	
    30	  switch (type) {
    31	    case 'decision': {
    32	      /** @type {{seq: number, ts: string, summary: any, detail?: any}} */
    33	      const entry = { seq, ts, summary: payload.summary };
    34	      if (payload.detail !== undefined) entry.detail = payload.detail;
    35	      out.decisions.push(entry);
    36	      break;
    37	    }
    38	    case 'note':
    39	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: payload.text });
    40	      // A structured StopFailure rate_limit note marks the open bundle
    41	      // limit-hit (gate-2 fix 3): the unsealed limit death must be visible to
    42	      // the next SessionStart on any platform, not buried in the decision log.
    43	      if (payload.structured?.kind === 'stop-failure' && payload.errorType === 'rate_limit' && out.handoff.status === 'open') {
    44	        out.handoff = { ...out.handoff, reason: out.handoff.reason ?? payload.text ?? null, reasonClass: 'usage-limit' };
    45	      }
    46	      break;
    47	    case 'plan.set':
    48	      if (Array.isArray(payload.steps) && payload.steps.every((/** @type {any} */ s) => s !== null && typeof s === 'object')) {
    49	        out.plan.steps = payload.steps;
    50	      } else {
    51	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.set', payload?.steps) });
    52	      }
    53	      break;
    54	    case 'plan.step': {
    55	      if (payload === null || typeof payload !== 'object' || typeof payload.id !== 'string') {
    56	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.step', payload) });
    57	        break;
    58	      }
    59	      const existing = out.plan.steps.find((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id === payload.id);
    60	      if (existing) Object.assign(existing, payload);
    61	      else out.plan.steps.push(payload);
    62	      break;
    63	    }
    64	    case 'file.touch': {
    65	      const safePath = jailRelPath(payload?.path);
    66	      if (safePath === null) {
    67	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('file.touch', payload?.path) });
    68	        break;
    69	      }
    70	      const entry = { path: safePath, op: typeof payload.op === 'string' ? payload.op : 'touch', lastTs: ts };
    71	      const i = out.files.touched.findIndex((/** @type {any} */ f) => f.path === safePath);
    72	      if (i === -1) out.files.touched.push(entry);
    73	      else out.files.touched[i] = entry;
    74	      break;
    75	    }
    76	    case 'roles.remap': {
    77	      // The assignments object AND every value must be non-null objects (iter-3
    78	      // F3): a well-formed {planner: null} used to be stored verbatim and then
    79	      // crashed renderHandoffMd. Untrusted journal input degrades to a note.
    80	      const a = payload?.assignments;
    81	      const wellFormed =
    82	        a !== null &&
    83	        typeof a === 'object' &&
    84	        !Array.isArray(a) &&
    85	        Object.values(a).every((/** @type {any} */ v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v));
    86	      if (wellFormed) {
    87	        out.roles.assignments = a;
    88	      } else {
    89	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('roles.remap', a) });
    90	      }
    91	      break;
    92	    }
    93	    case 'task.update': {
    94	      /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    95	      const patch = {};
    96	      if (typeof payload?.goal === 'string') patch.goal = payload.goal;
    97	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.constraints) && payload.constraints.every((/** @type {any} */ c) => typeof c === 'string')) {
    98	        patch.constraints = payload.constraints;
    99	      }
   100	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.acceptance)) patch.acceptance = payload.acceptance;
   101	      if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
   102	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('task.update', payload) });
   103	        break;
   104	      }
   105	      out.task = { ...out.task, ...patch };
   106	      break;
   107	    }
   108	    case 'git.update':
   109	      if (payload === null || typeof payload === 'object') out.git = payload;
   110	      else out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('git.update', payload) });
   111	      break;
   112	    case 'bundle.seed':
   113	      // Self-applying identity (gate-2 iter-2 B6): the auto-seed emits this as
   114	      // the journal's first event so a journal-only rebuild (snapshot + .bak
   115	      // both gone) restores the bundle's identity/origin/task instead of the
   116	      // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. Replayed first (lowest seq),
   117	      // so later events layer on top; on normal replay its seq is below the
   118	      // snapshot's journalSeq and it is skipped.
   119	      if (payload && typeof payload === 'object') {
   120	        if (typeof payload.bundleId === 'string') out.bundleId = payload.bundleId;
   121	        if (typeof payload.generation === 'number') out.generation = payload.generation;
   122	        if (typeof payload.createdAt === 'string') out.createdAt = payload.createdAt;
   123	        // Restore identity ONLY (platform/model) — never sessionHint/unstable.
   124	        // Session ownership is adopted AFTER the seed (a snapshot-only mutation),
   125	        // so replaying the seed must not clobber a later-adopted hint back to
   126	        // its seed-time null (gate-2 iter-2: caught by the adoption test).
   127	        if (payload.origin && typeof payload.origin === 'object') {
   128	          if (typeof payload.origin.platform === 'string') out.origin.platform = payload.origin.platform;
   129	          if (typeof payload.origin.model === 'string') out.origin.model = payload.origin.model;
   130	        }
   131	        if (payload.task && typeof payload.task === 'object' && typeof payload.task.goal === 'string') {
   132	          out.task.goal = payload.task.goal;
   133	        }
   134	      }
   135	      break;
   136	    case 'transcript.set':
   137	      // Opt-in transcript tail (plan §Transcript policy): stored under the
   138	      // literal property name `transcript` so purge-transcript strips it from
   139	      // every retained copy. Render never includes it.
   140	      if (payload?.transcript !== null && typeof payload?.transcript === 'object' && typeof payload.transcript.tail === 'string') {
   141	        out.transcript = {
   142	          capturedAt: typeof payload.transcript.capturedAt === 'string' ? payload.transcript.capturedAt : ts,
   143	          tail: payload.transcript.tail,
   144	        };
   145	      } else {
   146	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('transcript.set', 'malformed payload') });
   147	      }
   148	      break;
   149	    default:
   150	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: `[unhandled event type '${type}' recorded as note]` });
   151	      break;
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  out.updatedAt = ts;
   155	  // Harden against foreign/keyless entries (gate-2 fix): a missing seq must
   156	  // never poison journalSeq to NaN, and an absent dedupeKey must never enter
   157	  // the ring (undefined in the ring dedupes every later keyless event).
   158	  if (typeof seq === 'number' && Number.isFinite(seq)) out.journalSeq = Math.max(out.journalSeq, seq);
   159	  if (typeof event.dedupeKey === 'string' && event.dedupeKey.length > 0) {
   160	    out.dedupeRing.push(event.dedupeKey);
   161	    if (out.dedupeRing.length > RING_CAP) out.dedupeRing.splice(0, out.dedupeRing.length - RING_CAP);
   162	  }
   163	
   164	  return out;
   165	}

    46	/** Parse `git worktree list --porcelain` into [{path, branch}]. @param {string} out */
    47	function parseWorktreeList(out) {

    49	  const entries = [];

    51	    const path = block.match(/^worktree (.+)$/m)?.[1];
    52	    if (!path) continue;

    54	    entries.push({ path, branch });
    55	  }

    59	/**
    60	 * Ensure both seat worktrees exist on their namespaced branches and that

    65	export async function setupWorktrees(root, io) {
    66	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
    67	
    68	  const gitignorePath = `${root}/.gitignore`;
    69	  const existing = io.fs.existsSync(gitignorePath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(gitignorePath, 'utf8') : '';
    70	  const ensured = ensureIgnoreLine(existing, '.worktrees/');
    71	  if (ensured.changed) io.fs.writeFileSync(gitignorePath, ensured.text);
    72

    86	  }

    90	/**
    91	 * Preflight before a child runs in a seat: the worktree is listed, its index
    92	 * is clean, and HEAD sits on the expected branch. Refusals name the failed
    93	 * check and never issue destructive git.
    94	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
    95	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
    96	 */
    97	export async function preflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
    98	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
    99	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   100	  if (!listed.some((e) => e.path === path)) {
   101	    return { ok: false, check: 'missing-worktree', refusal: `worktree ${path} is not listed by git — run setup or self-heal first` };
   102	  }
   103	  const status = (await git(io, path, ['status', '--porcelain'])).stdout.trim();
   104	  if (status.length > 0) {
   105	    return { ok: false, check: 'dirty-index', refusal: `worktree ${path} has a dirty index/working tree — a clean seat is required before a child runs` };
   106	  }
   107	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   108	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   109	    return { ok: false, check: 'wrong-branch', refusal: `worktree ${path} HEAD is on '${head}', expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   110	  }
   111	  return { ok: true };
   112	}
   113	
   114	/**
   115	 * Postflight after a child: HEAD still on the expected branch (checked in the
   116	 * seat cwd) and main unmoved from the pre-child capture.
   117	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string, mainSha: string}} target @param {any} io
   118	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
   119	 */
   120	export async function postflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
   121	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   122	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   123	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   124	    return { ok: false, check: 'head-drift', refusal: `the child left HEAD on '${head}' instead of the expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   125	  }
   126	  const main = (await git(io, root, ['rev-parse', 'main'])).stdout.trim();
   127	  if (main !== target.mainSha) {
   128	    return { ok: false, check: 'main-moved', refusal: `main moved under the child (was ${target.mainSha}, now ${main}) — only the merger writes main` };
   129	  }
   130	  return { ok: true };
   131	}

   133	/**
   134	 * Full-range attribution scan over main..branch — the merge gate. Every
   135	 * commit must be authored AND committed by the sole author, with no forbidden
   136	 * AI-attribution trailer in the body. The range is never windowed.
   137	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   138	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, offending: string, reason: string}>}
   139	 */
   140	export async function attributionScan(root, branch, io) {
   141	  const out = (await git(io, root, ['log', `main..${branch}`, `--format=${LOG_FORMAT}`])).stdout;
   142	  for (const record of String(out).split('\x1e')) {
   143	    if (record.trim().length === 0) continue;
   144	    const [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body = ''] = record.replace(/^\n/, '').split('\x00');
   145	    if (an !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ae !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   146	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign author identity (${an} <${ae}>) — sole-author is required` };
   147	    }
   148	    if (cn !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ce !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   149	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign committer identity (${cn} <${ce}>) — sole-author is required` };
   150	    }
   151	    if (TRAILER_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(body))) {
   152	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} carries a forbidden AI-attribution trailer (co-authored/generated-with/emoji) in its message` };
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	  return { ok: true };
   156	}
   157	
   158	const mergeLockPath = (/** @type {string} */ root) => `${root}/.handoff/loop/merge.lock`;
   159	
   160	/**
   161	 * Merge an approved subtask branch into main — one guarded transaction:
   162	 * acquire the merge lock (a held lock refuses), gate on the attribution scan,
   163	 * merge at the repo root, abort-and-park on conflict, then ff-only sync both
   164	 * seats. The lock is released on EVERY exit path after acquisition.
   165	 * @param {string} root @param {{branch: string}} input @param {any} io
   166	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, branch: string} | {ok: false, reason: string}>}
   167	 */
   168	export async function mergeSubtask(root, input, io) {
   169	  const lock = mergeLockPath(root);
   170	  if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) {
   171	    return { ok: false, reason: `the merge lock at ${lock} is held by another merger — refusing a concurrent merge` };
   172	  }
   173	  ensureDir(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/loop`);
   174	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lock, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, at: io.now() });
   175	  try {
   176	    const scan = await attributionScan(root, input.branch, io);
   177	    if (scan.ok !== true) return { ok: false, reason: scan.reason };
   178	
   179	    try {
   180	      await git(io, root, ['merge', input.branch]);
   181	    } catch (err) {
   182	      // Conflict (or any merge failure): abort, never auto-resolve — park.
   183	      try {
   184	        await git(io, root, ['merge', '--abort']);
   185	      } catch {
   186	        // Nothing to abort — the failure predates a merge state.
   187	      }
   188	      const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stdout || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   189	      return { ok: false, reason: `merge conflict — aborted and parked for the operator: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
   193	    for (const seat of SEATS) {
   194	      try {
   195	        await git(io, seats[seat], ['merge', '--ff-only', 'main']);
   196	      } catch (err) {
   197	        const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stderr || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   198	        return { ok: false, reason: `worktree wt-${seat} could not fast-forward to main — corruption signal, parked: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	    return { ok: true, branch: input.branch };
   202	  } finally {
   203	    try {
   204	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lock);
   205	    } catch {
   206	      // Best effort — a leaked lock is visible and operator-recoverable.
   207	    }
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	/**
   212	 * Self-heal a raw-deleted seat: git still lists it but the directory is gone
   213	 * — prune the stale metadata and re-add on the same branch.
   214	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
   215	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, healed: boolean}>}
   216	 */
   217	export async function selfHealWorktree(root, target, io) {
   218	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   219	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   220	  const stale = listed.some((e) => e.path === path) && !io.fs.existsSync(path);
   221	  if (!stale) return { ok: true, healed: false };
   222	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'prune']);
   223	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', path, target.branch]);
   224	  return { ok: true, healed: true };
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Tear down BOTH seats: remove the worktrees and delete their namespaced
   229	 * branches. Anything outside .worktrees/wt-* or baton/wt-* is never touched,
   230	 * so a teardown leaves the repo a plain git repo (acceptance constraint 5).
   231	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   232	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, removed: string[]}>}
   233	 */
   234	export async function teardownWorktrees(root, io) {
   235	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
   236	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   237	  /** @type {string[]} */
   238	  const removed = [];
   239	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
   240	    const entry = listed.find((e) => e.path === p.seats[seat]);
   241	    if (!entry) continue;
   242	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'remove', '--force', p.seats[seat]]);
   243	    if (entry.branch && WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(entry.branch)) {
   244	      await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', entry.branch]);
   245	    }
   246	    removed.push(seat);
   247	  }
   248	  return { ok: true, removed };
   249	}
   250	
   251	/**
   252	 * Branch jail: only baton/wt-* branches may ever be deleted through this
   253	 * layer. An out-of-namespace ref refuses without touching git.
   254	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   255	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, error: string}>}
   256	 */
   257	export async function deleteBranch(root, branch, io) {
   258	  if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) {
   259	    return { ok: false, error: `refused: '${branch}' is outside the baton/wt- namespace — the loop never deletes non-loop branches` };
   260	  }
   261	  await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
   262	  return { ok: true };
   263	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,
     3	 * mirroring the bundle store's crash-tolerance idioms (atomic tmp+rename
     4	 * snapshots, torn-tail-tolerant NDJSON, seq-dedup replay). Everything lives
     5	 * under <root>/.handoff/loop/; the supervisor is the sole writer. Plan:
     6	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop",
     7	 * §"Smoke gate", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery", §"Caps".
     8	 */
     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
    12	
    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
    14	
    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
    21	  DONE: 'done',
    22	});
    23	
    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
    28	  PARK: 'park',
    29	  RESUME: 'resume',
    30	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
    31	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
    32	});
    33	
    34	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
    35	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
    36	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
    37	
    38	/** @param {string} root */
    39	export function loopPaths(root) {
    40	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
    41	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
    42	}
    43	
    44	/**
    45	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
    46	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
    47	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
    48	 */
    49	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
    50	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
    51	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
    52	  const iterations = {};
    53	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
    54	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
    55	  }
    56	  return {
    57	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
    58	    runId,
    59	    goal: spec.goal,
    60	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
    61	    phaseIndex: 0,
    62	    iterations,
    63	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
    64	    parkReason: null,
    65	    escalation: null,
    66	    smokeApproval: null,
    67	    createdAt: io.now(),
    68	    journalSeq: 0,
    69	  };
    70	}
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
    74	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
    75	 * only produced for a real transition).
    76	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
    77	 */
    78	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
    79	  switch (ev?.type) {
    80	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
    81	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
    82	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
    83	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
    84	    }
    85	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
    86	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
    87	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
    88	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
    89	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
    90	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
    91	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
    92	      }
    93	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
    94	    }
    95	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
    96	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
    97	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
    98	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
    99	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
   100	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
   101	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
   102	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
   103	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
   104	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
   105	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
   106	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
   107	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
   108	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
   109	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
   110	    }
   111	    default:
   112	      return state;
   113	  }
   114	}
   115	
   116	/**
   117	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
   118	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
   119	 */
   120	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
   121	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   122	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   123	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
   124	}
   125	
   126	/**
   127	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
   128	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
   129	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io
   130	 * @returns {Promise<number>} the persisted seq
   131	 */
   132	export async function appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io) {
   133	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   134	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   135	  let seq = ev.seq;
   136	  if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   137	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   138	    const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   139	    let tailSeq = 0;
   140	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   141	      for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   142	        if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   143	      }
   144	    }
   145	    seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   146	  }
   147	  const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   148	  const entry = {
   149	    ...ev,
   150	    seq,
   151	    ts,
   152	    dedupeKey: typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0 ? ev.dedupeKey : dedupeKey({ seq, ts, type: ev.type, gate: ev.gate ?? null }),
   153	  };
   154	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, entry);
   155	  return seq;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/**
   159	 * Load = snapshot + torn-tail-tolerant replay of journal events PAST the
   160	 * snapshot's seq. Duplicate seqs/dedupeKeys apply exactly once, so a
   161	 * supervisor-death recovery never re-runs a completed iteration.
   162	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   163	 * @returns {Promise<{state: any, warnings: string[]}>}
   164	 */
   165	export async function loadLoopState(root, io) {
   166	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   167	  /** @type {string[]} */
   168	  const warnings = [];
   169	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   170	  if (!snap.ok) return { state: null, warnings: ['no loop state snapshot found'] };
   171	  let state = snap.value;
   172	  const baseSeq = typeof state.journalSeq === 'number' ? state.journalSeq : 0;
   173	
   174	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   175	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   176	    for (const w of jw ?? []) warnings.push(`journal: ${w.kind ?? 'warning'} at line ${w.line ?? '?'}`);
   177	    const applied = new Set();
   178	    let maxSeq = baseSeq;
   179	    for (const e of entries) {
   180	      const seq = typeof e.seq === 'number' ? e.seq : null;
   181	      if (seq !== null && seq <= baseSeq) continue; // already folded into the snapshot
   182	      const key = e.dedupeKey ?? (seq !== null ? `seq:${seq}` : null);
   183	      if (key !== null && applied.has(key)) continue; // replayed duplicate — apply once
   184	      if (seq !== null && applied.has(`seq:${seq}`)) continue;
   185	      state = applyLoopEvent(state, e);
   186	      if (key !== null) applied.add(key);
   187	      if (seq !== null) {
   188	        applied.add(`seq:${seq}`);
   189	        if (seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
   190	      }
   191	    }
   192	    state = { ...state, journalSeq: maxSeq };
   193	  }
   194	  return { state, warnings };
   195	}
   196	
   197	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   198	// Smoke-approval token — a STRUCTURED digest over the six bound inputs, so a
   199	// stale approval is refused NAMING what moved (mirrors the receive receipt
   200	// token's driftedInputs discipline).
   201	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202	
   203	const SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX = 'smk1';
   204	const SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
   205	  'stateDigest',
   206	  'smokeCmd',
   207	  'smokeOutputDigest',
   208	  'gitHead',
   209	  'gitContentDigest',
   210	  'childAssignment',
   211	]);
   212	
   213	/**
   214	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
   215	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
   216	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
   217	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   218	 * @returns {string}
   219	 */
   220	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
   221	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
   222	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
   223	  }
   224	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
   229	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
   230	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   231	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
   232	 */
   233	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
   234	  /** @type {string} */
   235	  let current;
   236	  try {
   237	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   238	  } catch {
   239	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
   240	  }
   241	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
   242	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
   243	  const b = current.split('.');
   244	  /** @type {string[]} */
   245	  const drifted = [];
   246	  SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.forEach((f, i) => {
   247	    if (a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]) drifted.push(f);
   248	  });
   249	  return { ok: false, driftedInputs: drifted.length > 0 ? drifted : [...SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS] };
   250	}

    36	function version() {
    37	  const pkgPath = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'package.json');
    38	  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(pkgPath, 'utf8')).version;
    39	}
    40	
    41	const USAGE = `usage: baton <command> [options]
    42	
    43	commands:
    44	  ${COMMANDS.join(', ')}
    45	
    46	global options:
    47	  --version   print version
    48	  --json      machine-readable output: one compact JSON envelope on stdout
    49	`;
    50	
    51	/**
    52	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
    53	 * @param {any} io
    54	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env
    55	 */
    56	function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
    57	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
    58	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');
    59	}
    60	
    61	/**
    62	 * @param {string[]} argv process args after the binary
    63	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, stdout, stderr}
    64	 * @returns {number | Promise<number>} exit code
    65	 */
    66	export function run(argv, io) {
    67	  const args = argv.filter((a) => a !== '');
    68	  // Global --json intent is parsed BEFORE dispatch (gate-2 iter-2 M6): a
    69	  // top-level `--json` with no command, or --help/--version, must still emit
    70	  // exactly one envelope rather than raw text.
    71	  const jsonWanted = args.includes('--json');
    72	  const cmdIndex = args.findIndex((a) => !a.startsWith('--'));
    73	  const cmd = cmdIndex === -1 ? undefined : args[cmdIndex];
    74	
    75	  if (cmd === undefined) {
    76	    // No command — just flags (or nothing).
    77	    if (args.includes('--version')) {
    78	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { version: version() } });
    79	      else io.stdout.write(version() + '\n');
    80	      return 0;
    81	    }
    82	    if (args.includes('--help')) {
    83	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage: USAGE.trim() } });
    84	      else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
    85	      return 0;
    86	    }
    87	    // Bare invocation (or a lone --json): a usage error.
    88	    if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'no command given' } });
    89	    else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
    90	    return 2;
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  // All flags (before and after the command token) reach the command; only the
    94	  // command token itself is stripped, so a leading `--json` isn't lost.
    95	  const rest = args.filter((_, i) => i !== cmdIndex);
    96	
    97	  // Usage errors honor the --json envelope contract too (gate-2 fix 11 + M6:
    98	  // global --json counts, wherever it sits).
    99	  const wantsJson = jsonWanted;
   100	  if (cmd === 'wrap') {
   101	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1' } });
   102	    else io.stderr.write(`baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1.\n${USAGE}`);
   103	    return 2;
   104	  }
   105	  if (!COMMANDS.includes(cmd)) {
   106	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: `unknown command '${cmd}'` } });
   107	    else io.stderr.write(`baton: unknown command '${cmd}'\n${USAGE}`);
   108	    return 2;
   109	  }
   110	
   111	  if (rest.includes('--help')) {
   112	    // Subcommand help honors the global --json envelope too (iter-3 F8): with
   113	    // --json it must emit exactly one envelope, never raw usage text.
   114	    const usage = `usage: baton ${cmd} [options]\n\nSee README for the ${cmd} contract.`;
   115	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage } });
   116	    else io.stdout.write(`${usage}\n`);
   117	    return 0;
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  if (cmd === 'status') return cmdStatus(rest, io);
   121	  if (cmd === 'detect') return cmdDetect(rest, io);
   122	  if (cmd === 'remap') return cmdRemap(rest, io);
   123	  if (cmd === 'checkpoint') return cmdCheckpoint(rest, io);
   124	  if (cmd === 'finalize') return cmdFinalize(rest, io);
   125	  if (cmd === 'receive') return cmdReceive(rest, io);
   126	  if (cmd === 'init') return cmdInit(rest, io);
   127	  if (cmd === 'doctor') return cmdDoctor(rest, io);
   128	  if (cmd === 'purge-transcript') return cmdPurgeTranscript(rest, io);
   129	  if (cmd === 'session-start') return cmdSessionStart(rest, io);
   130	  if (cmd === 'recover') return cmdRecover(rest, io);
   131	  if (cmd === 'loop') return cmdLoop(rest, io);
   132	
   133	  const error = { code: 'not-implemented', msg: `baton ${cmd}: not implemented yet` };
   134	  if (rest.includes('--json')) {
   135	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });
   136	  } else {
   137	    io.stderr.write(`${error.msg}\n`);
   138	  }
   139	  return 1;
   140	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Loop spec (baton/loop@1) — pure load/validate for the Layer-2 goal loop.
     3	 * The spec is committed project config (loop.json at the repo root); run
     4	 * state lives elsewhere (.handoff/loop/). Plan:
     5	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Loop spec".
     6	 */
     7	
     8	export const LOOP_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop@1';
     9	
    10	// The 5-cap is a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md §Review gates): a spec
    11	// asking for a 6th gate iteration is invalid, not a preference.
    12	export const ITERATION_CAP_MAX = 5;
    13	
    14	const DEFAULT_BUDGETS = Object.freeze({ iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 });
    15	
    16	/**
    17	 * The scaffold `baton loop init` writes. Phase ids are progress labels;
    18	 * every role is a matrix role ID resolved via baton.config.json — never a
    19	 * model name.
    20	 * @param {string} goal
    21	 * @returns {any}
    22	 */
    23	export function defaultLoopSpec(goal) {
    24	  return {
    25	    schema: LOOP_SCHEMA,
    26	    goal,
    27	    constraints: [],
    28	    smoke: { cmd: null, expect: null },
    29	    phases: [
    30	      { id: 'plan', role: 'planner' },
    31	      { id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' },
    32	      // The smoke slice is itself a TDD mini-cycle (Gate-1 iter-2 finding 2):
    33	      // failing smoke assertion → verifier approval → minimal implementation.
    34	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
    35	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
    36	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
    37	      { id: 'subtask-tests', role: 'test-author' },
    38	      { id: 'subtask-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
    39	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
    40	      { id: 'subtask-review', role: 'subtask-reviewer' },
    41	      { id: 'gate-2-a', role: 'final-reviewer-a' },
    42	      { id: 'gate-2-b', role: 'final-reviewer-b' },
    43	    ],
    44	    budgets: { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS },
    45	  };
    46	}
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * Validate a loop spec against the role matrix. Reports EVERY problem at
    50	 * once (each error names its offender) so a spec author fixes one round, not
    51	 * one field per run. Valid specs are returned normalized: caller-provided
    52	 * fields are preserved verbatim; only ABSENT constraints/budgets/smoke are
    53	 * filled with defaults.
    54	 * @param {any} spec
    55	 * @param {any} config the parsed baton.config.json (roles are checked against it)
    56	 * @returns {{ok: true, spec: any} | {ok: false, errors: string[]}}
    57	 */
    58	export function validateLoopSpec(spec, config) {
    59	  /** @type {string[]} */
    60	  const errors = [];
    61	  const s = spec ?? {};
    62	
    63	  if (s.schema !== LOOP_SCHEMA) errors.push(`schema must be '${LOOP_SCHEMA}' (got ${JSON.stringify(s.schema)})`);
    64	  if (typeof s.goal !== 'string' || s.goal.length === 0) errors.push('goal must be a non-empty string');
    65	
    66	  let constraints = s.constraints;
    67	  if (constraints === undefined) constraints = [];
    68	  if (!Array.isArray(constraints)) {
    69	    errors.push('constraints must be an array of strings');
    70	  } else {
    71	    constraints.forEach((c, i) => {
    72	      if (typeof c !== 'string') errors.push(`constraints[${i}] must be a string (got ${JSON.stringify(c)})`);
    73	    });
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  const roles = config?.roles ?? {};
    77	  if (!Array.isArray(s.phases) || s.phases.length === 0) {
    78	    errors.push('phases must be a non-empty array of {id, role}');
    79	  } else {
    80	    s.phases.forEach((/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    81	      if (typeof p?.id !== 'string' || p.id.length === 0) errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing an id (a non-empty progress label)`);
    82	      if (typeof p?.role !== 'string' || p.role.length === 0) {
    83	        errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing a role (a baton.config.json role ID)`);
    84	      } else if (!(p.role in roles)) {
    85	        errors.push(`phases[${i}]: unknown role '${p.role}' — add it to baton.config.json roles or fix the reference`);
    86	      }
    87	    });
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  let budgets = s.budgets;
    91	  if (budgets === undefined) {
    92	    budgets = { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS };
    93	  } else if (typeof budgets !== 'object' || budgets === null) {
    94	    errors.push('budgets must be an object');
    95	  } else {
    96	    const cap = budgets.iterationCap;
    97	    if (typeof cap !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(cap) || cap < 1 || cap > ITERATION_CAP_MAX) {
    98	      errors.push(`budgets.iterationCap must be a number between 1 and ${ITERATION_CAP_MAX} (the 5-iteration cap is a hard invariant; got ${JSON.stringify(cap)})`);
    99	    }
   100	    for (const field of ['perRoleTimeoutMin', 'maxChildrenPerPhase']) {
   101	      const v = budgets[field];
   102	      if (typeof v !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) {
   103	        errors.push(`budgets.${field} must be a positive number (got ${JSON.stringify(v)})`);
   104	      }
   105	    }
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  let smoke = s.smoke;
   109	  if (smoke === undefined) {
   110	    smoke = { cmd: null, expect: null };
   111	  } else if (typeof smoke !== 'object' || smoke === null) {
   112	    errors.push('smoke must be an object ({cmd, expect}, each a string or null)');
   113	  } else {
   114	    if (!(smoke.cmd === null || typeof smoke.cmd === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.cmd must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.cmd)})`);
   115	    if (!(smoke.expect === null || typeof smoke.expect === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.expect must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.expect)})`);
   116	  }
   117	
   118	  if (errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, errors };
   119	  return { ok: true, spec: { ...s, constraints, budgets, smoke } };
   120	}

    27	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    28	
    29	// Flags that consume a value — needed to split positionals from flag tokens
    30	// without mistaking a flag's value for a positional. Keep in sync with the
    31	// strict spec below plus the implied common string flag (--root).
    32	const STRING_FLAGS = new Set(['root', 'approve-smoke']);
    33	
    34	/**
    35	 * `baton loop <subcommand>` — the Layer-2 goal-loop surface. v1 subcommands:
    36	 * `init "<goal>"` (scaffold loop.json; two-phase, idempotent, dry-run-able).
    37	 * Supervisor-side by design: the BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD guard deliberately
    38	 * does NOT apply here.
    39	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    40	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    41	 */
    42	export async function cmdLoop(args, io) {
    43	  /** @type {string[]} */
    44	  const positionals = [];
    45	  /** @type {string[]} */
    46	  const flagTokens = [];
    47	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
    48	    const a = args[i];
    49	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
    50	      flagTokens.push(a);
    51	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
    52	        i += 1;
    53	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
    54	      }
    55	    } else {
    56	      positionals.push(a);
    57	    }
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, { 'dry-run': 'boolean', 'approve-smoke': 'string' });
    61	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'loop', parsed.error);
    62	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    63	
    64	  const sub = positionals[0];
    65	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton loop init "<goal>" or baton loop run');
    66	  if (sub === 'run') {
    67	    if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
    68	    if (flags['approve-smoke'] === '') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', '--approve-smoke requires a value (the token from smoke-approval.json)');
    69	    return runLoop(flags, io);
    70	  }
    71	  if (sub !== 'init') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: init, run)`);
    72	
    73	  const goal = positionals[1];
    74	  if (goal === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop init requires a goal — baton loop init "<goal>"');
    75	  if (positionals.length > 2) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[2]}'`);
    76	
    77	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    78	  const path = `${root}/loop.json`;
    79	
    80	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {string} */ human) => {
    81	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
    82	    else io.stdout.write(`${human}\n`);
    83	    return 0;
    84	  };
    85	
    86	  // Idempotent: an existing spec is the user's — never overwritten.
    87	  if (io.fs.existsSync(path)) {
    88	    return finish({ created: false, path }, `baton loop init: ${path} already exists — left untouched`);
    89	  }
    90	  if (flags['dry-run'] === true) {
    91	    return finish(
    92	      { created: false, path, dryRun: true },
    93	      `baton loop init (dry-run): would write ${path} — the default baton/loop@1 spec with goal "${goal}"`,
    94	    );
    95	  }
    96	  ensureDir(io.fs, root);
    97	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, path, defaultLoopSpec(goal));
    98	  return finish({ created: true, path }, `baton loop init: wrote ${path} (goal "${goal}") — edit constraints/smoke, then run baton loop run`);
    99	}
   100	
   101	// Exit codes for `loop run` (frozen by the loop-run test contract):
   102	// 0 done / awaiting-smoke-approval · 1 live-lock refusal · 2 usage error ·
   103	// 3 escalated · 4 parked.
   104	const EXIT_ESCALATED = 3;
   105	const EXIT_PARKED = 4;
   106	
   107	/** @param {any} g */
   108	const gitHeadOf = (g) => g?.headSha ?? 'none';
   109	/** @param {any} g */
   110	const gitContentOf = (g) => g?.contentDigest ?? dedupeKey(g ?? null);
   111	
   112	/**
   113	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
   114	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
   115	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
   116	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
   117	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
   118	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
   119	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   120	 */
   121	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
   122	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   123	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
   124	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   125	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   126	
   127	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
   128	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
   129	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
   130	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
   131	  }
   132	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
   133	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
   134	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
   135	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
   136	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
   137	  }
   138	  const spec = validated.spec;
   139	
   140	  // Supervisor run lock — same provably-dead discipline as the bundle lock:
   141	  // a live same-host owner is refused; a dead one is reclaimed.
   142	  const existingLock = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
   143	  if (existingLock.ok && existingLock.value && typeof existingLock.value.pid === 'number') {
   144	    const other = existingLock.value;
   145	    const alive = typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid) : true;
   146	    const sameProcess = other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
   147	    if (!sameProcess && (other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
   148	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: another supervisor (pid ${other.pid} on ${other.host}, run ${other.runId}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
   149	      return 1;
   150	    }
   151	    io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other.pid})\n`);
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  // Load or initialize the run state.
   155	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
   156	  if (state === null) {
   157	    state = initLoopState(spec, io);
   158	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   159	  }
   160	  const runId = state.runId;
   161	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   162	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lockPath, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId });
   163	
   164	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
   165	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
   166	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
   167	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
   168	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
   169	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   170	    return state;
   171	  };
   172	
   173	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
   174	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
   175	  const sessionHint = `loop-${runId}`;
   176	  /** @type {string[]} */
   177	  let avoid = [];
   178	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
   179	  let avoidEntries = [];
   180	  let childSeq = 0;
   181	
   182	  try {
   183	    // --approve-smoke: verify the presented token against RECOMPUTED inputs;
   184	    // drift parks (the human approved a state that no longer exists).
   185	    if (typeof flags['approve-smoke'] === 'string') {
   186	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   187	        return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `--approve-smoke only applies while the run awaits smoke approval (status: ${state.status})`);
   188	      }
   189	      const record = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`);
   190	      if (!record.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'no smoke-approval.json found — run the loop to the smoke gate first');
   191	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   192	      const currentInputs = {
   193	        stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   194	        smokeCmd: String(spec.smoke?.cmd ?? ''),
   195	        smokeOutputDigest: String(record.value?.inputs?.smokeOutputDigest ?? ''),
   196	        gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   197	        gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
   198	        childAssignment: String(record.value?.inputs?.childAssignment ?? ''),
   199	      };
   200	      const verify = verifySmokeToken(String(flags['approve-smoke']), currentInputs);
   201	      if (verify.ok !== true) {
   202	        const drifted = 'driftedInputs' in verify ? verify.driftedInputs.join(', ') : 'unknown';
   203	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `smoke approval token is stale — drifted inputs: ${drifted}; re-run the smoke gate` });
   204	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: smoke approval REFUSED — inputs drifted since the token was issued (${drifted}); the run is parked for a fresh smoke pass\n`);
   205	        return EXIT_PARKED;
   206	      }
   207	      await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: flags['approve-smoke'], verified: true });
   208	      io.stdout.write('baton loop run: smoke approval verified — continuing the run\n');
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   212	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is escalated (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   213	      return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   214	    }
   215	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
   216	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is parked — ${state.parkReason ?? 'no reason recorded'}\n`);
   217	      return EXIT_PARKED;
   218	    }
   219	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   220	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: awaiting smoke approval — resume with: baton loop run --approve-smoke <token from ${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json>\n`);
   221	      return 0;
   222	    }
   223	
   224	    // ---- The phase loop. ---------------------------------------------------
   225	    while (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING && state.phaseIndex < state.phaseCount) {
   226	      const phase = spec.phases[state.phaseIndex];
   227	      const cap = spec.budgets.iterationCap;
   228	      let findings = '';
   229	      let failoverAttempt = 0;
   230	      /** @type {any | null} */
   231	      let forcedAssignment = null;
   232	      /** @type {string | null} */
   233	      let forcedPrompt = null;
   234	      let phaseDone = false;
   235	
   236	      while (!phaseDone) {
   237	        // The cap gates the SPAWN: at the cap the reducer escalates and a
   238	        // 6th child never starts.
   239	        if ((state.iterations?.[phase.id] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   240	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   241	          atomicWriteText(
   242	            io.fs,
   243	            `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   244	            `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n\nResolve the findings, then resume with baton loop run.\n`,
   245	          );
   246	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
   247	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   248	        }
   249	        if (childSeq >= spec.budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase * spec.phases.length) {
   250	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: 'total child budget exhausted' });
   251	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   252	        }
   253	
   254	        const resolved =
   255	          forcedAssignment ??
   256	          (() => {
   257	            const r = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid, avoidEntries, probes: null }).assignments[phase.role];
   258	            return r && r.mode !== 'unavailable' ? r : null;
   259	          })();
   260	        if (!resolved) {
   261	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `no eligible platform/model for role '${phase.role}'` });
   262	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: no eligible assignment for role '${phase.role}' — parked\n`);
   263	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   264	        }
   265	        const assignment = { ...resolved, role: phase.role };
   266	        const prompt =
   267	          forcedPrompt ??
   268	          [
   269	            `You are the ${phase.role} for loop run ${runId}, phase '${phase.id}'.`,
   270	            `Goal: ${spec.goal}`,
   271	            spec.constraints.length > 0 ? `Constraints:\n${spec.constraints.map((/** @type {string} */ c) => `- ${c}`).join('\n')}` : '',
   272	            findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED with these findings — address every one:\n${findings}` : '',
   273	            "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   274	          ]
   275	            .filter(Boolean)
   276	            .join('\n\n');
   277	        forcedAssignment = null;
   278	        forcedPrompt = null;
   279	
   280	        childSeq += 1;
   281	        const logPath = `${p.dir}/children/${String(childSeq).padStart(3, '0')}-${phase.id}.log`;
   282	        ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
   283	        const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root });
   284	        const result = await runner(childSpec, {
   285	          timeoutMs: spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin * 60_000,
   286	          graceMs: 10_000,
   287	          logPath,
   288	          maxLogBytes: 1_000_000,
   289	          platform: assignment.platform,
   290	        });
   291	
   292	        // Classification reads the child's LOG (the frozen transcript), so a
   293	        // limit banner routes to failover even when a verdict parsed.
   294	        const transcript = io.fs.existsSync(logPath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8') : '';
   295	        const cls = classify({ text: transcript, exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 0, platform: assignment.platform, table }).class;
   296	
   297	        if (cls === 'usage-limit' || cls === 'model-unavailable' || cls === 'other-error' || cls === 'throttle' || cls === 'auth') {
   298	          failoverAttempt += 1;
   299	          const decision = await runFailover({
   300	            root,
   301	            io,
   302	            config,
   303	            role: phase.role,
   304	            assignment,
   305	            transcript,
   306	            exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 1,
   307	            sessionHint,
   308	            probes: null,
   309	            avoid,
   310	            avoidEntries,
   311	            attempt: failoverAttempt,
   312	            loopState: { runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
   313	          });
   314	          if (decision.action === 'park') {
   315	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: decision.reason });
   316	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: parked — ${decision.reason}${'resumeAt' in decision && decision.resumeAt ? ` (resume around ${decision.resumeAt})` : ''}\n`);
   317	            return EXIT_PARKED;
   318	          }
   319	          if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
   320	            if (decision.class === 'usage-limit') avoid = avoid.includes(assignment.platform) ? avoid : [...avoid, assignment.platform];
   321	            if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) avoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
   322	            forcedAssignment = { ...decision.assignment, role: phase.role };
   323	            forcedPrompt = typeof decision.prompt === 'string' && decision.prompt.length > 0 ? decision.prompt : null;
   324	            continue; // relaunch the same phase on the new assignment
   325	          }
   326	          continue; // retry: re-spawn the same phase once more
   327	        }
   328	
   329	        const verdict = String(result.verdict ?? 'BLOCKED');
   330	        if (verdict === 'APPROVED' || verdict === 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   331	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   332	          phaseDone = true;
   333	
   334	          // The smoke gate fires right after the smoke-build phase completes.
   335	          if (phase.id === 'smoke-build' && typeof spec.smoke?.cmd === 'string' && spec.smoke.cmd.length > 0) {
   336	            const [cmd, ...cmdArgs] = spec.smoke.cmd.split(/\s+/);
   337	            let smokeOut = '';
   338	            try {
   339	              const r = await io.execFile(cmd, cmdArgs, { cwd: root });
   340	              smokeOut = `${r?.stdout ?? ''}${r?.stderr ?? ''}`;
   341	            } catch (err) {
   342	              smokeOut = `smoke command failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err)}`;
   343	            }
   344	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT });
   345	            const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   346	            const inputs = {
   347	              stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   348	              smokeCmd: spec.smoke.cmd,
   349	              smokeOutputDigest: dedupeKey(smokeOut),
   350	              gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   351	              gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
   352	              childAssignment: JSON.stringify({ platform: assignment.platform, model: assignment.model }),
   353	            };
   354	            const token = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   355	            atomicWriteJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`, { token, inputs, issuedAt: io.now() });
   356	            atomicWriteText(
   357	              io.fs,
   358	              `${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`,
   359	              `# Smoke gate — human approval required\n\nRun: ${runId}\nCommand: \`${spec.smoke.cmd}\`\nExpected: ${spec.smoke.expect ?? '(unspecified)'}\n\n## Output\n\n\`\`\`\n${smokeOut}\n\`\`\`\n\nApprove with:\n\n    baton loop run --approve-smoke ${token}\n\nThe token is bound to the current state/output/git — any drift refuses it.\n`,
   360	            );
   361	            io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: smoke gate reached — review ${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md and approve with --approve-smoke <token>\n`);
   362	            return 0;
   363	          }
   364	        } else {
   365	          // BLOCKED (or unparseable, already coerced to BLOCKED upstream).
   366	          findings = String(result.findings ?? '');
   367	          const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict });
   368	          if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   369	            atomicWriteText(
   370	              io.fs,
   371	              `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   372	              `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
   373	            );
   374	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' escalated at the cap — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   375	            return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   376	          }
   377	        }
   378	      }
   379	    }
   380	
   381	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE || state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount) {
   382	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: done — every phase completed (run ${runId})\n`);
   383	      return 0;
   384	    }
   385	    io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: stopped in status ${state.status}\n`);
   386	    return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED ? EXIT_ESCALATED : 0;
   387	  } finally {
   388	    // The lock is per-invocation: release on every exit path so a paused run
   389	    // (awaiting approval) can be resumed by the next invocation.
   390	    try {
   391	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   392	    } catch {
   393	      // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the provably-dead path.
   394	    }
   395	  }
   396	}

codex
The current router still only dispatches `loop`; `pipeline` is intentionally absent at this red stage, so the command tests are testing a future routing surface through `run()`. I’m now reading the exact plan text and any locked decision notes so I can separate plan mismatch from implementation absence.
