  1174	/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba core/src/loop/state.mjs' in /Users/saksham/baton
  1175	 succeeded in 0ms:
  1176	     1	/**
  1177	     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,
  1178	     3	 * mirroring the bundle store's crash-tolerance idioms (atomic tmp+rename
  1179	     4	 * snapshots, torn-tail-tolerant NDJSON, seq-dedup replay). Everything lives
  1180	     5	 * under <root>/.handoff/loop/; the supervisor is the sole writer. Plan:
  1181	     6	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop",
  1182	     7	 * §"Smoke gate", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery", §"Caps".
  1183	     8	 */
  1184	     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
  1185	    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
  1186	    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
  1187	    12	
  1188	    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
  1189	    14	
  1190	    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
  1191	    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
  1192	    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
  1193	    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
  1194	    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
  1195	    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
  1196	    21	  DONE: 'done',
  1197	    22	});
  1198	    23	
  1199	    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
  1200	    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
  1201	    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
  1202	    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
  1203	    28	  PARK: 'park',
  1204	    29	  RESUME: 'resume',
  1205	    30	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
  1206	    31	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
  1207	    32	});
  1208	    33	
  1209	    34	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
  1210	    35	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
  1211	    36	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
  1212	    37	
  1213	    38	/** @param {string} root */
  1214	    39	export function loopPaths(root) {
  1215	    40	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
  1216	    41	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
  1217	    42	}
  1218	    43	
  1219	    44	/**
  1220	    45	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
  1221	    46	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
  1222	    47	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
  1223	    48	 */
  1224	    49	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
  1225	    50	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
  1226	    51	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
  1227	    52	  const iterations = {};
  1228	    53	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
  1229	    54	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
  1230	    55	  }
  1231	    56	  return {
  1232	    57	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
  1233	    58	    runId,
  1234	    59	    goal: spec.goal,
  1235	    60	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
  1236	    61	    phaseIndex: 0,
  1237	    62	    iterations,
  1238	    63	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
  1239	    64	    parkReason: null,
  1240	    65	    escalation: null,
  1241	    66	    smokeApproval: null,
  1242	    67	    createdAt: io.now(),
  1243	    68	    journalSeq: 0,
  1244	    69	  };
  1245	    70	}
  1246	    71	
  1247	    72	/**
  1248	    73	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
  1249	    74	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
  1250	    75	 * only produced for a real transition).
  1251	    76	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
  1252	    77	 */
  1253	    78	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
  1254	    79	  switch (ev?.type) {
  1255	    80	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
  1256	    81	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
  1257	    82	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
  1258	    83	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
  1259	    84	    }
  1260	    85	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
  1261	    86	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
  1262	    87	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
  1263	    88	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
  1264	    89	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
  1265	    90	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
  1266	    91	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
  1267	    92	      }
  1268	    93	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
  1269	    94	    }
  1270	    95	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
  1271	    96	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
  1272	    97	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
  1273	    98	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
  1274	    99	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
  1275	   100	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
  1276	   101	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
  1277	   102	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
  1278	   103	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
  1279	   104	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
  1280	   105	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
  1281	   106	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
  1282	   107	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
  1283	   108	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
  1284	   109	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
  1285	   110	    }
  1286	   111	    default:
  1287	   112	      return state;
  1288	   113	  }
  1289	   114	}
  1290	   115	
  1291	   116	/**
  1292	   117	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
  1293	   118	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
  1294	   119	 */
  1295	   120	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
  1296	   121	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  1297	   122	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  1298	   123	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
  1299	   124	}
  1300	   125	
  1301	   126	/**
  1302	   127	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
  1303	   128	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
  1304	   129	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io
  1305	   130	 * @returns {Promise<number>} the persisted seq
  1306	   131	 */
  1307	   132	export async function appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io) {
  1308	   133	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  1309	   134	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  1310	   135	  let seq = ev.seq;
  1311	   136	  if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
  1312	   137	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
  1313	   138	    const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  1314	   139	    let tailSeq = 0;
  1315	   140	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1316	   141	      for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  1317	   142	        if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
  1318	   143	      }
  1319	   144	    }
  1320	   145	    seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
  1321	   146	  }
  1322	   147	  const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
  1323	   148	  const entry = {
  1324	   149	    ...ev,
  1325	   150	    seq,
  1326	   151	    ts,
  1327	   152	    dedupeKey: typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0 ? ev.dedupeKey : dedupeKey({ seq, ts, type: ev.type, gate: ev.gate ?? null }),
  1328	   153	  };
  1329	   154	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, entry);
  1330	   155	  return seq;
  1331	   156	}
  1332	   157	
  1333	   158	/**
  1334	   159	 * Load = snapshot + torn-tail-tolerant replay of journal events PAST the
  1335	   160	 * snapshot's seq. Duplicate seqs/dedupeKeys apply exactly once, so a
  1336	   161	 * supervisor-death recovery never re-runs a completed iteration.
  1337	   162	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  1338	   163	 * @returns {Promise<{state: any, warnings: string[]}>}
  1339	   164	 */
  1340	   165	export async function loadLoopState(root, io) {
  1341	   166	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  1342	   167	  /** @type {string[]} */
  1343	   168	  const warnings = [];
  1344	   169	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
  1345	   170	  if (!snap.ok) return { state: null, warnings: ['no loop state snapshot found'] };
  1346	   171	  let state = snap.value;
  1347	   172	  const baseSeq = typeof state.journalSeq === 'number' ? state.journalSeq : 0;
  1348	   173	
  1349	   174	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1350	   175	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
  1351	   176	    for (const w of jw ?? []) warnings.push(`journal: ${w.kind ?? 'warning'} at line ${w.line ?? '?'}`);
  1352	   177	    const applied = new Set();
  1353	   178	    let maxSeq = baseSeq;
  1354	   179	    for (const e of entries) {
  1355	   180	      const seq = typeof e.seq === 'number' ? e.seq : null;
  1356	   181	      if (seq !== null && seq <= baseSeq) continue; // already folded into the snapshot
  1357	   182	      const key = e.dedupeKey ?? (seq !== null ? `seq:${seq}` : null);
  1358	   183	      if (key !== null && applied.has(key)) continue; // replayed duplicate — apply once
  1359	   184	      if (seq !== null && applied.has(`seq:${seq}`)) continue;
  1360	   185	      state = applyLoopEvent(state, e);
  1361	   186	      if (key !== null) applied.add(key);
  1362	   187	      if (seq !== null) {
  1363	   188	        applied.add(`seq:${seq}`);
  1364	   189	        if (seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
  1365	   190	      }
  1366	   191	    }
  1367	   192	    state = { ...state, journalSeq: maxSeq };
  1368	   193	  }
  1369	   194	  return { state, warnings };
  1370	   195	}
  1371	   196	
  1372	   197	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1373	   198	// Smoke-approval token — a STRUCTURED digest over the six bound inputs, so a
  1374	   199	// stale approval is refused NAMING what moved (mirrors the receive receipt
  1375	   200	// token's driftedInputs discipline).
  1376	   201	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1377	   202	
  1378	   203	const SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX = 'smk1';
  1379	   204	const SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
  1380	   205	  'stateDigest',
  1381	   206	  'smokeCmd',
  1382	   207	  'smokeOutputDigest',
  1383	   208	  'gitHead',
  1384	   209	  'gitContentDigest',
  1385	   210	  'childAssignment',
  1386	   211	]);
  1387	   212	
  1388	   213	/**
  1389	   214	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
  1390	   215	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
  1391	   216	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
  1392	   217	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
  1393	   218	 * @returns {string}
  1394	   219	 */
  1395	   220	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
  1396	   221	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
  1397	   222	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
  1398	   223	  }
  1399	   224	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
  1400	   225	}
  1401	   226	
  1402	   227	/**
  1403	   228	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
  1404	   229	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
  1405	   230	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
  1406	   231	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
  1407	   232	 */
  1408	   233	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
  1409	   234	  /** @type {string} */
  1410	   235	  let current;
  1411	   236	  try {
  1412	   237	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
  1413	   238	  } catch {
  1414	   239	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
  1415	   240	  }
  1416	   241	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
  1417	   242	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
  1418	   243	  const b = current.split('.');
  1419	   244	  /** @type {string[]} */
  1420	   245	  const drifted = [];
  1421	   246	  SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.forEach((f, i) => {
  1422	   247	    if (a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]) drifted.push(f);
  1423	   248	  });
  1424	   249	  return { ok: false, driftedInputs: drifted.length > 0 ? drifted : [...SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS] };
  1425	   250	}

  1480	    51	/**
  1481	    52	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
  1482	    53	 * @param {any} io
  1483	    54	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env
  1484	    55	 */
  1485	    56	function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
  1486	    57	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
  1487	    58	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');
  1488	    59	}
  1489	    60	
  1490	    61	/**
  1491	    62	 * @param {string[]} argv process args after the binary
  1492	    63	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, stdout, stderr}
  1493	    64	 * @returns {number | Promise<number>} exit code
  1494	    65	 */
  1495	    66	export function run(argv, io) {
  1496	    67	  const args = argv.filter((a) => a !== '');
  1497	    68	  // Global --json intent is parsed BEFORE dispatch (gate-2 iter-2 M6): a
  1498	    69	  // top-level `--json` with no command, or --help/--version, must still emit
  1499	    70	  // exactly one envelope rather than raw text.
  1500	    71	  const jsonWanted = args.includes('--json');
  1501	    72	  const cmdIndex = args.findIndex((a) => !a.startsWith('--'));
  1502	    73	  const cmd = cmdIndex === -1 ? undefined : args[cmdIndex];
  1503	    74	
  1504	    75	  if (cmd === undefined) {
  1505	    76	    // No command — just flags (or nothing).

  1507	    78	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { version: version() } });

  1509	    80	      return 0;
  1510	    81	    }
  1511	    82	    if (args.includes('--help')) {
  1512	    83	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage: USAGE.trim() } });
  1513	    84	      else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
  1514	    85	      return 0;
  1515	    86	    }
  1516	    87	    // Bare invocation (or a lone --json): a usage error.
  1517	    88	    if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'no command given' } });
  1518	    89	    else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
  1519	    90	    return 2;
  1520	    91	  }
  1521	    92

  1958	     4	 * state lives elsewhere (.handoff/loop/). Plan:
  1959	     5	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Loop spec".
  1960	     6	 */
  1961	     7	
  1962	     8	export const LOOP_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop@1';
  1963	     9	
  1964	    10	// The 5-cap is a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md §Review gates): a spec
  1965	    11	// asking for a 6th gate iteration is invalid, not a preference.
  1966	    12	export const ITERATION_CAP_MAX = 5;
  1967	    13	
  1968	    14	const DEFAULT_BUDGETS = Object.freeze({ iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 });
  1969	    15	
  1970	    16	/**
  1971	    17	 * The scaffold `baton loop init` writes. Phase ids are progress labels;
  1972	    18	 * every role is a matrix role ID resolved via baton.config.json — never a
  1973	    19	 * model name.
  1974	    20	 * @param {string} goal
  1975	    21	 * @returns {any}
  1976	    22	 */
  1977	    23	export function defaultLoopSpec(goal) {
  1978	    24	  return {
  1979	    25	    schema: LOOP_SCHEMA,
  1980	    26	    goal,
  1981	    27	    constraints: [],
  1982	    28	    smoke: { cmd: null, expect: null },
  1983	    29	    phases: [
  1984	    30	      { id: 'plan', role: 'planner' },
  1985	    31	      { id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' },
  1986	    32	      // The smoke slice is itself a TDD mini-cycle (Gate-1 iter-2 finding 2):
  1987	    33	      // failing smoke assertion → verifier approval → minimal implementation.
  1988	    34	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
  1989	    35	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
  1990	    36	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
  1991	    37	      { id: 'subtask-tests', role: 'test-author' },
  1992	    38	      { id: 'subtask-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
  1993	    39	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
  1994	    40	      { id: 'subtask-review', role: 'subtask-reviewer' },
  1995	    41	      { id: 'gate-2-a', role: 'final-reviewer-a' },
  1996	    42	      { id: 'gate-2-b', role: 'final-reviewer-b' },
  1997	    43	    ],
  1998	    44	    budgets: { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS },
  1999	    45	  };
  2000	    46	}
  2001	    47	
  2002	    48	/**
  2003	    49	 * Validate a loop spec against the role matrix. Reports EVERY problem at
  2004	    50	 * once (each error names its offender) so a spec author fixes one round, not
  2005	    51	 * one field per run. Valid specs are returned normalized: caller-provided
  2006	    52	 * fields are preserved verbatim; only ABSENT constraints/budgets/smoke are
  2007	    53	 * filled with defaults.
  2008	    54	 * @param {any} spec
  2009	    55	 * @param {any} config the parsed baton.config.json (roles are checked against it)
  2010	    56	 * @returns {{ok: true, spec: any} | {ok: false, errors: string[]}}
  2011	    57	 */
  2012	    58	export function validateLoopSpec(spec, config) {
  2013	    59	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2014	    60	  const errors = [];
  2015	    61	  const s = spec ?? {};
  2016	    62	
  2017	    63	  if (s.schema !== LOOP_SCHEMA) errors.push(`schema must be '${LOOP_SCHEMA}' (got ${JSON.stringify(s.schema)})`);

  2027	    73	    });
  2028	    74	  }

  2038	    84	      } else if (!(p.role in roles)) {

  2040	    86	      }
  2041	    87	    });
  2042	    88	  }
  2043	    89

  2045	    91	  if (budgets === undefined) {
  2046	    92	    budgets = { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS };
  2047	    93	  } else if (typeof budgets !== 'object' || budgets === null) {
  2048	    94	    errors.push('budgets must be an object');

  2053	    99	    }

  2055	   101	      const v = budgets[field];
  2056	   102	      if (typeof v !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) {
  2057	   103	        errors.push(`budgets.${field} must be a positive number (got ${JSON.stringify(v)})`);
  2058	   104	      }
  2059	   105	    }
  2060	   106	  }
  2061	   107	
  2062	   108	  let smoke = s.smoke;
  2063	   109	  if (smoke === undefined) {
  2064	   110	    smoke = { cmd: null, expect: null };
  2065	   111	  } else if (typeof smoke !== 'object' || smoke === null) {
  2066	   112	    errors.push('smoke must be an object ({cmd, expect}, each a string or null)');
  2067	   113	  } else {
  2068	   114	    if (!(smoke.cmd === null || typeof smoke.cmd === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.cmd must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.cmd)})`);
  2069	   115	    if (!(smoke.expect === null || typeof smoke.expect === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.expect must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.expect)})`);
  2070	   116	  }
  2071	   117	
  2072	   118	  if (errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, errors };
  2073	   119	  return { ok: true, spec: { ...s, constraints, budgets, smoke } };
  2074	   120	}

  2108	    32	const STRING_FLAGS = new Set(['root', 'approve-smoke']);

  2110	    34	/**
  2111	    35	 * `baton loop <subcommand>` — the Layer-2 goal-loop surface. v1 subcommands:
  2112	    36	 * `init "<goal>"` (scaffold loop.json; two-phase, idempotent, dry-run-able).
  2113	    37	 * Supervisor-side by design: the BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD guard deliberately
  2114	    38	 * does NOT apply here.
  2115	    39	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  2116	    40	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2117	    41	 */
  2118	    42	export async function cmdLoop(args, io) {
  2119	    43	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2120	    44	  const positionals = [];
  2121	    45	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2122	    46	  const flagTokens = [];
  2123	    47	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  2124	    48	    const a = args[i];
  2125	    49	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
  2126	    50	      flagTokens.push(a);
  2127	    51	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
  2128	    52	        i += 1;
  2129	    53	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
  2130	    54	      }
  2131	    55	    } else {
  2132	    56	      positionals.push(a);
  2133	    57	    }
  2134	    58	  }
  2135	    59	
  2136	    60	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, { 'dry-run': 'boolean', 'approve-smoke': 'string' });
  2137	    61	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'loop', parsed.error);
  2138	    62	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  2139	    63	
  2140	    64	  const sub = positionals[0];
  2141	    65	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton loop init "<goal>" or baton loop run');
  2142	    66	  if (sub === 'run') {
  2143	    67	    if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
  2144	    68	    if (flags['approve-smoke'] === '') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', '--approve-smoke requires a value (the token from smoke-approval.json)');
  2145	    69	    return runLoop(flags, io);
  2146	    70	  }
  2147	    71	  if (sub !== 'init') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: init, run)`);
  2148	    72	
  2149	    73	  const goal = positionals[1];
  2150	    74	  if (goal === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop init requires a goal — baton loop init "<goal>"');
  2151	    75	  if (positionals.length > 2) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[2]}'`);
  2152	    76	
  2153	    77	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  2154	    78	  const path = `${root}/loop.json`;
  2155	    79	
  2156	    80	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {string} */ human) => {
  2157	    81	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
  2158	    82	    else io.stdout.write(`${human}\n`);
  2159	    83	    return 0;
  2160	    84	  };
  2161	    85	
  2162	    86	  // Idempotent: an existing spec is the user's — never overwritten.
  2163	    87	  if (io.fs.existsSync(path)) {
  2164	    88	    return finish({ created: false, path }, `baton loop init: ${path} already exists — left untouched`);
  2165	    89	  }
  2166	    90	  if (flags['dry-run'] === true) {
  2167	    91	    return finish(
  2168	    92	      { created: false, path, dryRun: true },

  2170	    94	    );
  2171	    95	  }
  2172	    96	  ensureDir(io.fs, root);
  2173	    97	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, path, defaultLoopSpec(goal));
  2174	    98	  return finish({ created: true, path }, `baton loop init: wrote ${path} (goal "${goal}") — edit constraints/smoke, then run baton loop run`);
  2175	    99	}
  2176	   100	
  2177	   101	// Exit codes for `loop run` (frozen by the loop-run test contract):
  2178	   102	// 0 done / awaiting-smoke-approval · 1 live-lock refusal · 2 usage error ·
  2179	   103	// 3 escalated · 4 parked.

  2181	   105	const EXIT_PARKED = 4;
  2182	   106	
  2183	   107	/** @param {any} g */

  2185	   109	/** @param {any} g */
  2186	   110	const gitContentOf = (g) => g?.contentDigest ?? dedupeKey(g ?? null);

  2188	   112	/**
  2189	   113	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
  2190	   114	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
  2191	   115	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
  2192	   116	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
  2193	   117	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
  2194	   118	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
  2195	   119	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2196	   120	 */
  2197	   121	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
  2198	   122	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  2199	   123	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
  2200	   124	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  2201	   125	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
  2202	   126	
  2203	   127	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
  2204	   128	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
  2205	   129	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
  2206	   130	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
  2207	   131	  }
  2208	   132	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
  2209	   133	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
  2210	   134	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
  2211	   135	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
  2212	   136	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
  2213	   137	  }
  2214	   138	  const spec = validated.spec;
  2215	   139	
  2216	   140	  // Supervisor run lock — same provably-dead discipline as the bundle lock:
  2217	   141	  // a live same-host owner is refused; a dead one is reclaimed.
  2218	   142	  const existingLock = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
  2219	   143	  if (existingLock.ok && existingLock.value && typeof existingLock.value.pid === 'number') {
  2220	   144	    const other = existingLock.value;
  2221	   145	    const alive = typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid) : true;
  2222	   146	    const sameProcess = other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
  2223	   147	    if (!sameProcess && (other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
  2224	   148	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: another supervisor (pid ${other.pid} on ${other.host}, run ${other.runId}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
  2225	   149	      return 1;
  2226	   150	    }
  2227	   151	    io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other.pid})\n`);
  2228	   152	  }
  2229	   153	
  2230	   154	  // Load or initialize the run state.
  2231	   155	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
  2232	   156	  if (state === null) {
  2233	   157	    state = initLoopState(spec, io);
  2234	   158	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  2235	   159	  }
  2236	   160	  const runId = state.runId;
  2237	   161	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  2238	   162	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lockPath, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId });
  2239	   163	
  2240	   164	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
  2241	   165	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
  2242	   166	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
  2243	   167	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
  2244	   168	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
  2245	   169	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  2246	   170	    return state;
  2247	   171	  };
  2248	   172	
  2249	   173	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
  2250	   174	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
  2251	   175	  const sessionHint = `loop-${runId}`;
  2252	   176	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2253	   177	  let avoid = [];
  2254	   178	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
  2255	   179	  let avoidEntries = [];
  2256	   180	  let childSeq = 0;
  2257	   181	
  2258	   182	  try {
  2259	   183	    // --approve-smoke: verify the presented token against RECOMPUTED inputs;
  2260	   184	    // drift parks (the human approved a state that no longer exists).
  2261	   185	    if (typeof flags['approve-smoke'] === 'string') {
  2262	   186	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
  2263	   187	        return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `--approve-smoke only applies while the run awaits smoke approval (status: ${state.status})`);
  2264	   188	      }

  2266	   190	      if (!record.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'no smoke-approval.json found — run the loop to the smoke gate first');
  2267	   191	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
  2268	   192	      const currentInputs = {
  2269	   193	        stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
  2270	   194	        smokeCmd: String(spec.smoke?.cmd ?? ''),
  2271	   195	        smokeOutputDigest: String(record.value?.inputs?.smokeOutputDigest ?? ''),
  2272	   196	        gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
  2273	   197	        gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
  2274	   198	        childAssignment: String(record.value?.inputs?.childAssignment ?? ''),
  2275	   199	      };
  2276	   200	      const verify = verifySmokeToken(String(flags['approve-smoke']), currentInputs);
  2277	   201	      if (verify.ok !== true) {
  2278	   202	        const drifted = 'driftedInputs' in verify ? verify.driftedInputs.join(', ') : 'unknown';
  2279	   203	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `smoke approval token is stale — drifted inputs: ${drifted}; re-run the smoke gate` });
  2280	   204	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: smoke approval REFUSED — inputs drifted since the token was issued (${drifted}); the run is parked for a fresh smoke pass\n`);
  2281	   205	        return EXIT_PARKED;

  2284	   208	      io.stdout.write('baton loop run: smoke approval verified — continuing the run\n');

  2287	   211	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
  2288	   212	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is escalated (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
  2289	   213	      return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  2290	   214	    }
  2291	   215	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {

  2297	   221	      return 0;

  2315	   239	        if ((state.iterations?.[phase.id] ?? 0) >= cap) {

  2321	   245	          );
  2322	   246	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
  2323	   247	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  2324	   248	        }
  2325	   249	        if (childSeq >= spec.budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase * spec.phases.length) {
  2326	   250	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: 'total child budget exhausted' });
  2327	   251	          return EXIT_PARKED;
  2328	   252	        }
  2329	   253	
  2330	   254	        const resolved =
  2331	   255	          forcedAssignment ??

  2333	   257	            const r = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid, avoidEntries, probes: null }).assignments[phase.role];
  2334	   258	            return r && r.mode !== 'unavailable' ? r : null;
  2335	   259	          })();
  2336	   260	        if (!resolved) {
  2337	   261	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `no eligible platform/model for role '${phase.role}'` });
  2338	   262	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: no eligible assignment for role '${phase.role}' — parked\n`);
  2339	   263	          return EXIT_PARKED;
  2340	   264	        }
  2341	   265	        const assignment = { ...resolved, role: phase.role };
  2342	   266	        const prompt =
  2343	   267	          forcedPrompt ??
  2344	   268	          [
  2345	   269	            `You are the ${phase.role} for loop run ${runId}, phase '${phase.id}'.`,
  2346	   270	            `Goal: ${spec.goal}`,
  2347	   271	            spec.constraints.length > 0 ? `Constraints:\n${spec.constraints.map((/** @type {string} */ c) => `- ${c}`).join('\n')}` : '',

  2350	   274	          ]

  2354	   278	        forcedPrompt = null;

  2358	   282	        ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
  2359	   283	        const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root });
  2360	   284	        const result = await runner(childSpec, {
  2361	   285	          timeoutMs: spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin * 60_000,
  2362	   286	          graceMs: 10_000,
  2363	   287	          logPath,
  2364	   288	          maxLogBytes: 1_000_000,
  2365	   289	          platform: assignment.platform,
  2366	   290	        });
  2367	   291	
  2368	   292	        // Classification reads the child's LOG (the frozen transcript), so a
  2369	   293	        // limit banner routes to failover even when a verdict parsed.
  2370	   294	        const transcript = io.fs.existsSync(logPath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8') : '';
  2371	   295	        const cls = classify({ text: transcript, exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 0, platform: assignment.platform, table }).class;
  2372	   296	
  2373	   297	        if (cls === 'usage-limit' || cls === 'model-unavailable' || cls === 'other-error' || cls === 'throttle' || cls === 'auth') {
  2374	   298	          failoverAttempt += 1;
  2375	   299	          const decision = await runFailover({

  2377	   301	            io,
  2378	   302	            config,
  2379	   303	            role: phase.role,
  2380	   304	            assignment,

  2385	   309	            avoid,
  2386	   310	            avoidEntries,
  2387	   311	            attempt: failoverAttempt,
  2388	   312	            loopState: { runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
  2389	   313	          });

  2400	   324	            continue; // relaunch the same phase on the new assignment
  2401	   325	          }
  2402	   326	          continue; // retry: re-spawn the same phase once more
  2403	   327	        }
  2404	   328	
  2405	   329	        const verdict = String(result.verdict ?? 'BLOCKED');
  2406	   330	        if (verdict === 'APPROVED' || verdict === 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
  2407	   331	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
  2408	   332	          phaseDone = true;
  2409	   333	
  2410	   334	          // The smoke gate fires right after the smoke-build phase completes.

  2412	   336	            const [cmd, ...cmdArgs] = spec.smoke.cmd.split(/\s+/);

  2415	   339	              const r = await io.execFile(cmd, cmdArgs, { cwd: root });
  2416	   340	              smokeOut = `${r?.stdout ?? ''}${r?.stderr ?? ''}`;
  2417	   341	            } catch (err) {
  2418	   342	              smokeOut = `smoke command failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err)}`;
  2419	   343	            }
  2420	   344	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT });
  2421	   345	            const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
  2422	   346	            const inputs = {
  2423	   347	              stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
  2424	   348	              smokeCmd: spec.smoke.cmd,
  2425	   349	              smokeOutputDigest: dedupeKey(smokeOut),
  2426	   350	              gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
  2427	   351	              gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
  2428	   352	              childAssignment: JSON.stringify({ platform: assignment.platform, model: assignment.model }),
  2429	   353	            };
  2430	   354	            const token = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
  2431	   355	            atomicWriteJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`, { token, inputs, issuedAt: io.now() });
  2432	   356	            atomicWriteText(

  2434	   358	              `${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`,
  2435	   359	              `# Smoke gate — human approval required\n\nRun: ${runId}\nCommand: \`${spec.smoke.cmd}\`\nExpected: ${spec.smoke.expect ??   2436	   360	            );
  2437	   361	            io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: smoke gate reached — review ${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md and approve with --approve-smoke <token>\n`);
  2438	   362	            return 0;
  2439	   363	          }
  2440	   364	        } else {
  2441	   365	          // BLOCKED (or unparseable, already coerced to BLOCKED upstream).
  2442	   366	          findings = String(result.findings ?? '');
  2443	   367	          const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict });
  2444	   368	          if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
  2445	   369	            atomicWriteText(
  2446	   370	              io.fs,
  2447	   371	              `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
  2448	   372	              `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
  2449	   373	            );
  2450	   374	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' escalated at the cap — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
  2451	   375	            return EXIT_ESCALATED;
  2452	   376	          }
  2453	   377	        }
  2454	   378	      }
  2455	   379	    }
  2456	   380	
  2457	   381	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE || state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount) {
  2458	   382	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: done — every phase completed (run ${runId})\n`);
  2459	   383	      return 0;
  2460	   384	    }
  2461	   385	    io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: stopped in status ${state.status}\n`);
  2462	   386	    return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED ? EXIT_ESCALATED : 0;
  2463	   387	  } finally {
  2464	   388	    // The lock is per-invocation: release on every exit path so a paused run
  2465	   389	    // (awaiting approval) can be resumed by the next invocation.
  2466	   390	    try {
  2467	   391	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
  2468	   392	    } catch {
  2469	   393	      // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the provably-dead path.
  2470	   394	    }
  2471	   395	  }

  2493	    15	## Locked decisions (Saksham, 2026-07-18)
  2494	    16	
  2495	    17	1. **Packaging**: inside the baton repo — new commands `baton loop` and

  2502	    24	   produce a minimal end-to-end slice (build + run + one real assertion) and

  2505	    27	4. **Driver**: a supervisor CLI — spawns headless role children

  2508	    30	
  2509	    31	## Design
  2510	    32	
  2511	    33	### Loop spec
  2512	    34	
  2513	    35	`loop.json` at repo root (schema `baton/loop@1`), scaffolded by
  2514	    36	`baton loop init "<goal>"`:
  2515	    37	
  2516	    38	- `goal`, `constraints[]` (falsifiable acceptance criteria — the loop's exit
  2517	    39	  condition), `smoke` ({cmd, expect} — the staged-gate slice definition),
  2518	    40	- `phases` — an ordered list of `{id, role}` entries: `id` is a free label
  2519	    41	  for progress display; `role` MUST be a matrix role ID (validation fails on
  2520	    42	  any unknown role before a child spawns). Default:
  2521	    43	  plan(`planner`) → gate-1(`plan-reviewer`) → smoke — itself a TDD mini-cycle
  2522	    44	  per the repo contract: smoke-tests(`test-author`) →
  2523	    45	  smoke-verify(`test-verifier`) → smoke-build(`implementer`) → the human
  2524	    46	  smoke gate — → per-subtask cycle (`test-author` → `test-verifier` →
  2525	    47	  `implementer` → `subtask-reviewer`) → gate-2(`final-reviewer-a`,
  2526	    48	  `final-reviewer-b`) → done,
  2527	    49	- `budgets` ({iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin, maxChildrenPerPhase}),
  2528	    50	- roles resolve through the existing `baton.config.json` matrix — never
  2529	    51	  hardcoded models.
  2530	    52	
  2531	    53	### Role-matrix additions (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 1)
  2532	    54	
  2533	    55	The loop references roles by ID only; every referenced role must exist in the
  2534	    56	matrix or `loop.json` validation fails (`unknown role '<id>'`, exit 2, before
  2535	    57	any child spawns). Four roles are ADDED to `baton.config.json` +
  2536	    58	`templates/baton.config.json.tpl` in the first implementation task:
  2537	    59	
  2538	    60	- `subtask-reviewer` — per-subtask diff review inside the loop cycle.
  2539	    61	- `merger` — the only role that writes main in pipeline mode; adversarial
  2540	    62	  re-check before merge.
  2541	    63	- `worker-a`, `worker-b` — the two pipeline seats. Subtask N assigns
  2542	    64	  writer = seat (N mod 2), reviewer = the other seat; the SEATS swap, and each
  2543	    65	  seat's concrete platform/model comes from its configured chain via the
  2544	    66	  resolver. No vendor or model name appears in this plan, child prompts, or
  2545	    67	  loop code — chains live in config only.
  2546	    68	
  2547	    69	### `baton loop` (Layer 2)
  2548	    70	
  2549	    71	- `baton loop init <goal>` — scaffold spec + plan template; idempotent,
  2550	    72	  dry-run-able, same two-phase plan/apply discipline as `baton init`.
  2551	    73	- `baton loop run [--approve-smoke <token>]` — the state machine. State lives
  2552	    74	  in gitignored `.handoff/loop/state.json` + `journal.ndjson` (same atomic
  2553	    75	  write + journal replay machinery as the bundle; loop position is also
  2554	    76	  checkpointed into the handoff bundle so a limit death carries it).
  2555	    77	
  2556	    78	### Root + ownership discipline (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 2)
  2557	    79	
  2558	    80	One authoritative root: the MAIN repo root where `loop.json` lives. The
  2559	    81	supervisor is the SOLE writer of `.handoff/loop/` and of the main root's
  2560	    82	handoff bundle; every supervisor-driven baton invocation passes explicit
  2561	    83	`--root <mainRoot>` and a supervisor-owned stable session hint
  2562	    84	(`--session loop-<runId>` — a NEW flag added to `checkpoint`; `receive`
  2563	    85	already has it), so the bundle has exactly one stable owner.
  2564	    86	
  2565	    87	**Child-hook policy (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 1; iteration 3,
  2566	    88	finding 1)** — children never touch OR read any bundle, by construction: the
  2567	    89	supervisor sets `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1` in every child's environment, and
  2568	    90	EVERY baton hook command — the hook entrypoints, `baton checkpoint`, and
  2569	    91	`baton session-start` (which would otherwise read the bundle and inject
  2570	    92	pending-handoff resume context into the child) — quietly no-ops (exit 0,
  2571	    93	empty output) when it is set. This covers
  2572	    94	Layer-2 children running in the MAIN root (where root discovery would
  2573	    95	otherwise find the supervisor-owned bundle and the foreign-session guard
  2574	    96	would reject their stable hints) and pipeline children in worktrees alike;
  2575	    97	narrative checkpointing for supervised work is the supervisor's job, driven
  2576	    98	by parsed child results. Contract tests: (a) Layer-2 — two concurrent
  2577	    99	main-root children with distinct stable session hints and live hooks →
  2578	   100	zero bundle mutations, zero foreign-session rejections, supervisor
  2579	   101	`--session` checkpoints land; (b) pipeline — same from two worktrees while
  2580	   102	the supervisor writes loop state.
  2581	   103	- Each role invocation is a headless child under the supervisor:
  2582	   104	  - claude-code roles: `claude -p "<role prompt>" --permission-mode
  2583	   105	    acceptEdits --allowedTools ...` (allowlist per role: reviewers read-only).
  2584	   106	  - codex roles: `codex exec -C <root> -s <sandbox> --model <m> -c
  2585	   107	    model_reasoning_effort=<e> "<prompt>" </dev/null` (stdin closed — the
  2586	   108	    stdin-hang class is a known field failure).
  2587	   109	  - Verdict contract: children end with the structured tail
  2588	   110	    (`VERDICT: APPROVED|APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|BLOCKED` + FINDINGS) already used
  2589	   111	    by every review gate; the supervisor parses exactly that.
  2590	   112	
  2591	   113	### Child supervision contract (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 7)
  2592	   114	
  2593	   115	`children.mjs` owns the full child lifecycle, not just argv assembly:
  2594	   116	
  2595	   117	- Spawn detached in its OWN process group; stdin closed at spawn (the
  2596	   118	  codex-exec stdin-hang class); per-role timeout → SIGTERM to the group, 10 s
  2597	   119	  grace, then SIGKILL to the group (no zombie grandchildren; reap verified in
  2598	   120	  tests with a child that forks a sleeper).
  2599	   121	- Full child output streams to `.handoff/loop/children/<child-id>.log` with a
  2600	   122	  byte cap (head preserved, middle truncated with a marker — the verdict tail
  2601	   123	  is at the END so the tail is never truncated).
  2602	   124	- **Verdict parsing is region-bounded (prompt-echo defense)**: only the
  2603	   125	  child's FINAL message region is parsed — claude-code children run with a
  2604	   126	  structured output format and the parser reads the result field; codex
  2605	   127	  children are parsed strictly AFTER the last `tokens used` marker. The
  2606	   128	  prompt region (which quotes `VERDICT:` in its instructions) is never
  2607	   129	  scanned. Multiple tails in the final region → the LAST one wins.
  2608	   130	- An unparseable, absent, or truncated verdict is BLOCKED-with-escalation,
  2609	   131	  never APPROVED; a child that exits after making edits but before a verdict
  2610	   132	  parks the subtask with its worktree preserved for inspection.
  2611	   133	- A generic nonzero exit classifies as `other-error` (existing classifier
  2612	   134	  rule) → the loop retries once, then parks; it NEVER triggers limit failover
  2613	   135	  without limit-specific evidence.
  2614	   136	- Fixture-driven tests: prompt echo, multiple verdict tails, truncated
  2615	   137	  transcript, timeout kill/reap, zombie grandchild, non-limit nonzero exit.
  2616	   138	
  2617	   139	### Supervisor lifetime and recovery (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 5)
  2618	   140	
  2619	   141	"Survives the operator's session ending" is a defined mechanism, not a hope:
  2620	   142	
  2621	   143	- `baton loop run` is FOREGROUND by default. `--detach` re-launches the
  2622	   144	  supervisor in its own session (`setsid`), output to
  2623	   145	  `.handoff/loop/supervisor.log`, and returns.
  2624	   146	- A run lock `.handoff/loop/supervisor.lock` carries {pid, process
  2625	   147	  start-time, host, runId} — the same provably-dead machinery as the
  2626	   148	  Layer-1 bundle lock. A second `loop run` against a LIVE supervisor is
  2627	   149	  refused; against a provably-dead one it recovers.
  2628	   150	- The state machine is journal-replayable: recovery replays
  2629	   151	  `journal.ndjson`, marks any in-flight child of the dead supervisor as
  2630	   152	  interrupted (its process group is gone or killed on adoption), and either
  2631	   153	  continues from the last completed step or parks with an escalation note —
  2632	   154	  never re-runs a completed gate iteration.
  2633	   155	- Acceptance adds a supervisor-death test: kill the supervisor (and its
  2634	   156	  launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
  2635	   157	  journal and continues or parks; no orphaned children survive (process
  2636	   158	  groups reaped on adoption).
  2637	   159	
  2638	   160	- **Smoke gate (staleness-proof — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 8; TDD order —
  2639	   161	  iteration 2, finding 2)**: after gate-1, the smoke slice is built as a TDD
  2640	   162	  mini-cycle (test-author writes the failing smoke assertion → test-verifier
  2641	   163	  approves it → implementer writes only enough to pass it — the repo TDD
  2642	   164	  contract applies to the slice, not just to subtasks); the supervisor then
  2643	   165	  runs `smoke.cmd`, records output, and STOPS, writing
  2644	   166	  `.handoff/loop/SMOKE-REVIEW.md` plus `smoke-approval.json` containing an
  2645	   167	  approval token = digest over {loop-state digest, smoke.cmd, output digest,
  2646	   168	  HEAD, content-sensitive git digest (existing snapshot machinery), child
  2647	   169	  assignment}. Resuming requires `baton loop run --approve-smoke <token>`;
  2648	   170	  the supervisor recomputes the digest first and REFUSES a drifted token
  2649	   171	  (anything changed since the human looked) — it parks for a fresh smoke run
  2650	   172	  instead. No full-run budget is spent before a valid approval.
  2651	   173	- **Limit failover (the baton integration — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 3)**:
  2652	   174	  an exact transaction, not a loose chain. On child death: (1) reap the
  2653	   175	  process group and freeze the transcript; (2) classify via `baton detect`;
  2654	   176	  non-limit → the retry/park path above. On a limit classification:
  2655	   177	  (3) checkpoint the loop position INTO THE BUNDLE (supervisor
  2656	   178	  `checkpoint --session loop-<runId>`) so the seal carries it and the receipt
  2657	   179	  token binds it; (4) seal via `finalize --reason-class usage-limit --root
  2658	   180	  <mainRoot>` (an already-dead/unsealed bundle takes the degraded-open path
  2659	   181	  receive already supports); (5) refresh probes; (6) `receive --prepare` then
  2660	   182	  `--commit` back-to-back with IDENTICAL intake flags, the supervisor session
  2661	   183	  hint, and the same explicit root, with NO intervening writes; (7) resolver
  2662	   184	  output (with the dead entry avoided) picks the relaunch platform+model;
  2663	   185	  (8) relaunch the child with the resume prompt. A stale-token rejection
  2664	   186	  triggers ONE automatic re-prepare/commit retry; a second failure parks.
  2665	   187	  Between prepare and commit the failover holds the loop transaction lock and
  2666	   188	  writes NOTHING — `.handoff/loop/` writes are not token-bound inputs, so
  2667	   189	  this is enforced by construction and ASSERTED in tests (fs spy: zero
  2668	   190	  `.handoff/loop/` writes inside the window), not assumed. Drift-rejection
  2669	   191	  tests inject the inputs the token actually binds: git change and probe
  2670	   192	  refresh between prepare and commit → stale-token → one retry path. Plus:
  2671	   193	  open-bundle (unsealed) limit death; the full seal→receive→relaunch happy
  2672	   194	  path.
  2673	   195	- **Model-level failover (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 4)**: the resolver
  2674	   196	  gains entry-level avoidance — `avoidEntries: [{platform, model}]` alongside
  2675	   197	  the existing platform-level `avoid[]` — and the classifier gains a
  2676	   198	  `model-unavailable` signature class (distinct from usage-limit; seeded with
  2677	   199	  the verified account-tier rejection string). A model rejected at spawn adds
  2678	   200	  its {platform, model} entry to avoidEntries and re-resolves: other models
  2679	   201	  on the SAME platform remain selectable. Named test: first chain entry
  2680	   202	  rejected as model-unavailable → the next entry on the same platform is
  2681	   203	  selected, with no platform-wide avoidance.
  2682	   204	  All platforms/entries exhausted → the loop parks with a resume-at hint
  2683	   205	  instead of dying.
  2684	   206	- **Caps**: every gate loops at most 5 iterations; on exhaustion the loop
  2685	   207	  parks and escalates (writes `.handoff/loop/ESCALATION.md`, notifies) — never
  2686	   208	  a 6th run.
  2687	   209	
  2688	   210	### `baton pipeline` (Layer 3 — a loop preset)
  2689	   211	
  2690	   212	- `baton pipeline run` = `loop run` with the dual-worktree subtask cycle:
  2691	   213	  - Two persistent worktrees `.worktrees/wt-a`, `.worktrees/wt-b`
  2692	   214	    (`git worktree add`); `pipeline init` ensure-lines `.worktrees/` into
  2693	   215	    `.gitignore` (same idempotent machinery as the `.handoff/` line).
  2694	   216	  - Subtask N: the writer seat implements on a namespaced branch
  2695	   217	    (`baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-N`) in its worktree; the reviewer seat reviews
  2696	   218	    the diff from the OTHER worktree (fresh checkout, fresh context). Seats
  2697	   219	    swap every subtask: worker-a writes / worker-b reviews → worker-b writes /
  2698	   220	    worker-a reviews → … (concrete models per seat come from the role
  2699	   221	    matrix — see Role-matrix additions).
  2700	   222	  - Verdict flow: APPROVED → the merger role adversarially re-checks and
  2701	   223	    merges into main (the ONLY writer to main); BLOCKED → writer fixes,
  2702	   224	    ≤5 iterations, then escalate. After each merge both worktrees
  2703	   225	    fast-forward.
  2704	   226	
  2705	   227	  **Worktree transaction layer (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 5)** —
  2706	   228	  `worktrees.mjs` treats every git operation as a guarded transaction:
  2707	   229	  - Branch namespace `baton/wt-*/…` is loop-owned; anything else is never
  2708	   230	    touched.
  2709	   231	  - Preflight before each child: worktree exists in `git worktree list`,
  2710	   232	    index clean, HEAD on the expected branch — a dirty or drifted worktree
  2711	   233	    parks the subtask instead of spawning.
  2712	   234	  - Postflight after each child: HEAD still on the expected branch, main
  2713	   235	    unmoved by the child — violation parks with the evidence.
  2714	   236	  - Merges run under a supervisor-held merge lock; worktree sync after merge
  2715	   237	    is ff-only (a non-ff sync is a corruption signal → park); merge conflicts
  2716	   238	    park + escalate, never auto-resolved.
  2717	   239	  - Stale metadata self-heals: a raw-deleted worktree directory is detected
  2718	   240	    (`git worktree list` vs fs), pruned (`git worktree prune`), and re-added;
  2719	   241	    teardown uses `git worktree remove` + branch deletion so acceptance
  2720	   242	    constraint 5 (no residue) holds.
  2721	   243	  - Adversarial tests: stale branch from a previous run, dirty worker,
  2722	   244	    main moved mid-subtask, merge conflict, raw-deleted `.worktrees/`,
  2723	   245	    untracked `.worktrees/` (missing gitignore line → doctor check).
  2724	   246	
  2725	   247	  **Attribution enforcement for child commits (Gate-1 iteration 1,
  2726	   248	  finding 6)** — the invariant is enforced, not assumed:
  2727	   249	  - The supervisor sets `GIT_AUTHOR_NAME/EMAIL` and `GIT_COMMITTER_NAME/EMAIL`
  2728	   250	    (sole author identity) in EVERY child's environment.
  2729	   251	  - Every merge is gated on an attribution scan over the exact range
  2730	   252	    `main..baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-N` — author/committer identity plus the
  2731	   253	    forbidden-trailer patterns (the existing doctor patterns, applied to the
  2732	   254	    full range, not a last-50 window). A violation blocks the merge and
  2733	   255	    parks.
  2734	   256	  - The existing doctor 50-commit scan remains the repo-wide backstop.
  2735	   257	
  2736	   258	### Module layout (all under `core/src/loop/`)
  2737	   259	
  2738	   260	`spec.mjs` (load/validate loop.json incl. role-existence check) · `state.mjs`
  2739	   261	(state machine + journal + smoke-approval token) · `children.mjs` (full child
  2740	   262	lifecycle: spawn/process-group/timeout/log-cap, region-bounded verdict parse,
  2741	   263	detect-on-exit) · `failover.mjs` (the limit-death transaction) ·
  2742	   264	`worktrees.mjs` (guarded git transactions, branch discipline, attribution
  2743	   265	range scan) · `commands/loop.mjs`, `commands/pipeline.mjs`. Plus three Layer-1
  2744	   266	extensions: `roles/resolve.mjs` gains `avoidEntries[]` (entry-level
  2745	   267	avoidance, backward-compatible — omitted means current behavior); the
  2746	   268	signature table gains the `model-unavailable` class; `checkpoint` gains a
  2747	   269	`--session <hint>` flag (supervisor identity), and the
  2748	   270	`BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD` no-op guard lands in the hook entrypoints,
  2749	   271	`checkpoint`, AND `session-start` (every hook-invoked command).
  2750	   272	
  2751	   273	### Acceptance constraints (the loop's exit condition)
  2752	   274	
  2753	   275	1. On a fixture repo, `baton loop run` drives a toy goal end-to-end headlessly:
  2754	   276	   plan → gate-1 → smoke slice AS A TDD MINI-CYCLE (failing smoke assertion
  2755	   277	   authored and verifier-approved BEFORE the slice implementation — order
  2756	   278	   asserted from the journal) → parked for approval → (approved) → 2 subtasks
  2757	   279	   TDD → gate-2 → done, with review artifacts under `reviews/` in the fixture.
  2758	   280	   Layer-2 children run in the main root with live hooks and neither mutate
  2759	   281	   NOR read the supervisor-owned bundle: under `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1`,
  2760	   282	   hook commands on all three platforms produce zero bundle writes AND zero
  2761	   283	   session-start resume context (both asserted).
  2762	   284	2. Simulated limit death (child exits with a verbatim limit banner) mid-subtask
  2763	   285	   → the loop seals, remaps the role off the dead platform, resumes on the
  2764	   286	   other harness, and completes the subtask. Chained A→B→A tested. Separately:
  2765	   287	   a model-unavailable rejection at spawn selects the NEXT entry on the same
  2766	   288	   platform (no platform-wide avoidance).
  2767	   289	3. `baton pipeline run` completes 2 subtasks with alternating writer/reviewer
  2768	   290	   across the two worktrees; the attribution range scan passes for main AND
  2769	   291	   both worker branch ranges (child commits included) — sole author, zero AI
  2770	   292	   attribution; reviewer children run read-only.
  2771	   293	4. The 5-cap parks + escalates on every gate (unit-forced exhaustion test);
  2772	   294	   no gate ever runs a 6th iteration. Killing the supervisor (and its
  2773	   295	   launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
  2774	   296	   journal and continues or parks, with no orphaned children.
  2775	   297	5. Deleting `.handoff/` and `.worktrees/` leaves the fixture repo exactly as
  2776	   298	   a plain git repo (no residue outside gitignored trees).
  2777	   299	6. All existing 878 tests stay green; the new suite covers spec validation,
  2778	   300	   state-machine transitions (unit, injected io), child verdict parsing
  2779	   301	   (fixture transcripts), failover (fake children), and worktree discipline.
  2780	   302	
  2781	   303	### Top risks
  2782	   304	
  2783	   305	1. **Headless permission surfaces** — Claude Code children need permission
  2784	   306	   flags that stay inside the sandboxed repo; reviewers get read-only
  2785	   307	   allowlists. Mitigation: per-role allowlist table + spike task 0.
  2786	   308	2. **Verdict-parse drift** — models sometimes wrap the tail; the
  2787	   309	   region-bounded parser (Child supervision contract) takes the LAST tail in
  2788	   310	   the final-message region only and treats an unparseable one as
  2789	   311	   BLOCKED-with-escalation, never as APPROVED.
  2790	   312	3. **Cost runaway** — per-phase child caps + iteration caps + the smoke gate
  2791	   313	   before any full-run spend; every child's token/duration logged to the loop
  2792	   314	   journal.
  2793	   315	4. **Worktree state divergence** — only the merger writes main; workers are
  2794	   316	   branch-jailed; ff-sync after each merge; `baton pipeline doctor` checks
  2795	   317	   worktree health.
  2796	   318	5. **Cross-platform child API drift** — the spawn contract lives in ONE module
  2797	   319	   (children.mjs) with per-platform fixtures, same seam discipline as
  2798	   320	   normalize.mjs.
  2799	   321	
  2800	   322	## Out of scope for this milestone
  2801	   323	
  2802	   324	Multi-repo loops; parallel subtask fan-out (>1 writer at once); cloud/cron
  2803	   325	driving (the supervisor is local-first; scheduled runs are a later layer);
  2804	   326	LLM-judged smoke verdicts (the smoke gate is a human gate by decision 3).
  2805	   327

     6	 * @returns {{config: any | null, errors: {path: string, msg: string}[]}}

     8	export function loadConfig(cwd, io) {
     9	  const file = `${cwd}/baton.config.json`;
    10	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
    11	  const errors = [];
    12	
    13	  let raw;
    14	  try {
    15	    raw = io.fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    16	  } catch {
    17	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json not found at ${file} — run 'baton init' to scaffold it` }] };
    18	  }
    19	  let parsed;
    20	  try {
    21	    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    22	  } catch (err) {
    23	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json is not valid JSON (${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'parse error'})` }] };
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  if (parsed.roles === null || typeof parsed.roles !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed.roles)) {
    27	    errors.push({ path: 'roles', msg: 'roles must be an object mapping role names to chain arrays' });
    28	  }
    29	
    30	  /** @type {Record<string, any[]>} */
    31	  const roles = {};
    32	  if (errors.length === 0) {
    33	    for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(parsed.roles)) {
    34	      if (!Array.isArray(chain)) {
    35	        errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}`, msg: 'a role chain must be an array of "platform/model[@effort]" strings' });
    36	        continue;
    37	      }
    38	      /** @type {any[]} */
    39	      const entries = [];
    40	      for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    41	        const s = chain[i];
    42	        const slash = typeof s === 'string' ? s.indexOf('/') : -1;
    43	        if (slash <= 0 || slash === s.length - 1) {
    44	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `malformed chain entry ${JSON.stringify(s)} — expected "platform/model[@effort]"` });
    45	          continue;
    46	        }
    47	        const platform = s.slice(0, slash);
    48	        const rest = s.slice(slash + 1);
    49	        const at = rest.indexOf('@');
    50	        const model = at === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, at);
    51	        const effort = at === -1 ? null : rest.slice(at + 1);
    52	        if (parsed.platforms && !(platform in parsed.platforms)) {
    53	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `unknown platform "${platform}" — not a key of config.platforms` });
    54	          continue;
    55	        }
    56	        entries.push({ platform, model, effort });
    57	      }
    58	      roles[role] = entries;
    59	    }
    60	  }
    61	
    62	  if (errors.length > 0) return { config: null, errors };
    63	  return { config: { ...parsed, roles }, errors };
    64	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>, nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], avoidEntries = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  // Entry-level avoidance (plan §Model-level failover): a specific
    54	  // {platform, model} tuple is dead (e.g. account-tier rejected) while its
    55	  // platform stays usable. Matching is on model IDENTITY — an @effort suffix
    56	  // on either side is ignored — and requires BOTH dimensions, so the same
    57	  // model name on another platform is never caught.
    58	  /** @param {any} m */
    59	  const baseModel = (m) => (typeof m === 'string' ? m.split('@')[0] : m);
    60	  /** @param {string} platform @param {string} model */
    61	  const entryAvoided = (platform, model) =>
    62	    avoidEntries.some((a) => a?.platform === platform && baseModel(a?.model) === baseModel(model));
    63	
    64	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    65	  const assignments = {};
    66	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    67	    /** @type {any[]} */
    68	    const skipped = [];
    69	    /** @type {any} */
    70	    let assignment = null;
    71	
    72	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    73	      const entry = chain[i];
    74	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    75	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    76	        continue;
    77	      }
    78	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    79	      if (why) {
    80	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    81	        continue;
    82	      }
    83	      // After skipReason so a dual match keeps the platform-level label.
    84	      if (entryAvoided(entry.platform, entry.model)) {
    85	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'avoided-entry' });
    86	        continue;
    87	      }
    88	      assignment = {
    89	        platform: entry.platform,
    90	        model: entry.model,
    91	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    92	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    93	        chainIndex: i,
    94	        skipped,
    95	      };
    96	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    97	      break;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
   101	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
   102	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
   103	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
   104	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
   105	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   106	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
   107	      }
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    if (!assignment) {
   111	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   112	    }
   113	    assignments[role] = assignment;
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  return { assignments, notes };
   117	}

    93	/**
    94	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
    95	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
    96	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
    97	 * spawn spec.
    98	 */
    99	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   100	  const queue = [...script];
   101	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   102	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   103	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   104	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   105	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   106	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   107	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   108	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   109	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   110	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   111	    }
   112	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   113	  };
   114	  fn.calls = calls;
   115	  return fn;
   116	}

   118	function makeLoopRepo({ spec = loopSpec(), files = {}, runner, execFile, env = {} } = {}) {
   119	  const io = makeIo({
   120	    now: T0,
   121	    host: 'loop-host',
   122	    pid: 4242,

   130	      ...files,
   131	    },
   132	  });
   133	  if (execFile) io.execFile = execFile;
   134	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   135	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   136	  io.__runner = fake;
   137	  return io;
   138	}

   255	    // Each retry carries the PRECEDING attempt's findings — later findings are
   256	    // not dropped in favor of the first.
   257	    assert.match(serialize(io, 1), /FINDING-ALPHA-1/, "the 2nd attempt's prompt carries the 1st attempt's findings");
   258	    assert.match(serialize(io, 2), /FINDING-BETA-2/, "the 3rd attempt's prompt carries the 2nd attempt's findings (continuity, not just the first)");
   259	
   260	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   261	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run escalated at the cap');
   262	    const esc = loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md');
   263	    assert.ok(esc, 'an ESCALATION.md was written under .handoff/loop');
   264	    assert.match(esc, /gate-1/, 'the escalation names the exhausted gate');
   265	  });
   266	});

   268	// ===========================================================================
   269	describe('loop run — smoke gate', () => {
   270	  const smokeSpec = loopSpec({
   271	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
   272	    phases: [
   273	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
   274	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
   275	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
   276	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
   277	    ],
   278	  });
   279	
   280	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
   281	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   282	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
   283	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
   284	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   285	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
   286	    };
   287	  }
   288	
   289	  it('after smoke-build: runs smoke.cmd, writes SMOKE-REVIEW.md + a smoke-approval token, awaits, exit 0, no further child', async () => {
   290	    const rec = [];
   291	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   292	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   293	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   294	
   295	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   296	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'pausing for smoke approval is a clean exit 0');
   297	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 3, 'the smoke slice ran (3 phases); the post-smoke phase did NOT spawn');
   298	    assert.ok(rec.some((c) => /smoke/.test(c)), 'smoke.cmd was executed via io.execFile');
   299	
   300	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md'), 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
   301	    const approval = loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json');
   302	    assert.ok(approval, 'a smoke-approval record was written');
   303	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
   304	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'the approval carries a smokeApprovalToken over the six inputs');
   305	
   306	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   307	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run is awaiting smoke approval');
   308	  });
   309	
   310	  it('--approve-smoke <correct token> resumes into the next phase (a child spawns again)', async () => {
   311	    const rec = [];
   312	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   313	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   314	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   315	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   316	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;
   317	
   318	    // Resume with the correct token — a FRESH runner for the continued phase.
   319	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   320	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   321	    io.__runner = runner2;
   322	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
   323	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   324	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 1, 'a verified approval continues into the post-smoke phase');
   325	  });
   326	
   327	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
   328	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
   329	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
   330	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
   331	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   332	      if (cmd === 'git') {
   333	        const key = args.join(' ');
   334	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
   335	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
   336	      }
   337	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
   338	    };
   339	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
   340	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   341	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   342	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   343	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
   344	
   345	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
   346	    head = 'HEAD-B';
   347	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   348	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   349	    io.__runner = runner2;
   350	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
   351	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
   352	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
   353	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   354	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
   355	  });
   356	});

   359	describe('loop run — failover integration', () => {
